FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/belief.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::ops::{Add, AddAssign, Mul};

/// Multidimensional belief state used by the Executive Cortex.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefVector {
    pub implementation_confidence: f32,
    pub evidence_quality: f32,
    pub architectural_risk: f32,
    pub security_confidence: f32,
    /// Absolute change from the previous update (used by residual scheduler).
    pub residual: f32,
    /// True once a concrete success marker has been observed.
    pub has_success_marker: bool,
    /// Bit-packed counter of verified artifacts (cache-friendly on ARM64).
    pub verified_artifacts: u8,
}

impl BeliefVector {
    pub const ZERO: Self = Self {
        implementation_confidence: 0.0,
        evidence_quality: 0.0,
        architectural_risk: 0.0,
        security_confidence: 0.0,
        residual: 0.0,
        has_success_marker: false,
        verified_artifacts: 0,
    };

    pub fn new(
        implementation_confidence: f32,
        evidence_quality: f32,
        architectural_risk: f32,
        security_confidence: f32,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            implementation_confidence: implementation_confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            evidence_quality: evidence_quality.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            architectural_risk: architectural_risk.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            security_confidence: security_confidence.clamp(0.0, 1.0),
            residual: 0.0,
            has_success_marker: false,
            verified_artifacts: 0,
        }
    }

    /// Hard penalty method when a Policy Denial / Security Violation occurs.
    pub fn penalize_security(mut self) -> Self {
        self.security_confidence = 0.0;
        self.implementation_confidence = 0.0;
        self.architectural_risk = 1.0;
        self.residual = 1.0;
        self
    }

    /// Scalar utility used by ranking logic.
    pub fn scalar_score(&self) -> f32 {
        let positive = (self.implementation_confidence * 0.40)
            + (self.evidence_quality * 0.35)
            + (self.security_confidence * 0.25);
        let penalty = self.architectural_risk * 0.45;
        (positive - penalty).clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }

    pub fn update(&self, other: &Self, weight: f32) -> Self {
        let w = weight.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let mut next = Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence * (1.0 - w) + other.implementation_confidence * w,
            self.evidence_quality * (1.0 - w) + other.evidence_quality * w,
            self.architectural_risk * (1.0 - w) + other.architectural_risk * w,
            self.security_confidence * (1.0 - w) + other.security_confidence * w,
        );
        next.residual = (self.scalar_score() - next.scalar_score()).abs();
        next.has_success_marker = self.has_success_marker || other.has_success_marker;
        next.verified_artifacts = self.verified_artifacts | other.verified_artifacts;
        next
    }

    /// Calibrated Bayesian update on the positive dimensions with damping.
    pub fn bayesian_update(&self, likelihood: f32, damping: f32) -> Self {
        let likelihood = likelihood.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        let damping = damping.clamp(0.0, 1.0);

        fn bayes(prior: f32, like: f32) -> f32 {
            let num = prior * like;
            let den = num + (1.0 - prior) * (1.0 - like);
            if den == 0.0 {
                prior
            } else {
                num / den
            }
        }

        let impl_post = bayes(self.implementation_confidence, likelihood);
        let evid_post = bayes(self.evidence_quality, likelihood);
        let sec_post = bayes(self.security_confidence, likelihood);

        let impl_d = damping * impl_post + (1.0 - damping) * self.implementation_confidence;
        let evid_d = damping * evid_post + (1.0 - damping) * self.evidence_quality;
        let sec_d = damping * sec_post + (1.0 - damping) * self.security_confidence;

        let mut next = Self::new(impl_d, evid_d, self.architectural_risk, sec_d);
        next.residual = (self.scalar_score() - next.scalar_score()).abs();
        next.has_success_marker = self.has_success_marker;
        next.verified_artifacts = self.verified_artifacts;
        next
    }

    /// Hard evidence gate. Belief alone is never enough.
    /// required_artifacts is a bitmask -- ALL required bits must actually be
    /// verified (bitwise AND), not merely a numerically-larger bit pattern.
    pub fn may_prove(&self, required_artifacts: u8, threshold: f32) -> bool {
        self.scalar_score() >= threshold
            && self.has_success_marker
            && (self.verified_artifacts & required_artifacts) == required_artifacts
    }

    /// Mark that a concrete success artifact was observed.
    pub fn mark_success(&mut self, artifact_bit: u8) {
        self.has_success_marker = true;
        self.verified_artifacts |= artifact_bit;
    }

    /// Convenience for residual-driven schedulers.
    pub fn scheduling_priority(&self, impact: f32, mission_priority: f32) -> f32 {
        self.residual * impact * mission_priority
    }
}

impl Default for BeliefVector {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(0.45, 0.30, 0.40, 0.55)
    }
}

impl Add for BeliefVector {
    type Output = Self;
    fn add(self, rhs: Self) -> Self {
        Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence + rhs.implementation_confidence,
            self.evidence_quality + rhs.evidence_quality,
            self.architectural_risk + rhs.architectural_risk,
            self.security_confidence + rhs.security_confidence,
        )
    }
}

impl AddAssign for BeliefVector {
    fn add_assign(&mut self, rhs: Self) {
        *self = *self + rhs;
    }
}

impl Mul<f32> for BeliefVector {
    type Output = Self;
    fn mul(self, rhs: f32) -> Self {
        Self::new(
            self.implementation_confidence * rhs,
            self.evidence_quality * rhs,
            self.architectural_risk * rhs,
            self.security_confidence * rhs,
        )
    }
}

/// Artifact bitflags for verified WASM-related evidence.
pub const ARTIFACT_WASM_COMPILED: u8 = 0b0000_0001;
pub const ARTIFACT_WASM_OPTIMIZED: u8 = 0b0000_0010;
pub const ARTIFACT_WASM_FUEL_OK: u8 = 0b0000_0100;

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evidence/types.rs 
=========================================
use crate::evidence::BeliefVector;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Explicit failure taxonomy to prevent infinite retry loops on security denials.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ExecutionFailureKind {
    /// Hard security / policy violation (e.g. PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED).
    /// MUST immediately zero out strategy belief and mark path as ineligible.
    PolicyDenial { path: String, reason: String },

    /// Target file/resource missing on disk (e.g., ENOENT os error 2).
    ResourceNotFound { target: String },

    /// Non-zero exit code or tool runtime failure.
    ExecutionError { exit_code: i32, stderr: String },

    /// Lock timeout or transient resource contention.
    TransientTimeout { resource: String },
}

/// Explicit provenance tracking for evidence claims.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProvenanceLevel {
    /// Directly verified via tool execution or file contents read from disk.
    Observed { byte_count: usize },

    /// Derived logically from AST symbols or dependency graphs.
    Inferred { basis_symbol: String, confidence: f32 },

    /// Unverified assumption or heuristic generated by model reasoning.
    Hypothesized { rationale: String },
}

impl Default for ProvenanceLevel {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::Hypothesized { rationale: "Unverified initial assumption".to_string() }
    }
}

/// Raw observation string coming out of the RoboticExecutor.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Observation {
    pub raw: String,
    pub source: String, // "executor", "grep", "read_file", "podman", etc.
    pub success: bool,
    pub latency_ms: u64,
    pub failure_kind: Option<ExecutionFailureKind>,
}

/// Semantic classification of an observation.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EvidenceKind {
    CommandSuccess,
    CommandFailure,
    FileRead,
    FileWrite,
    PatternMatch,
    SecurityViolation,
    ArchitecturalSmell,
    PositiveSignal,
    Neutral,
}

/// Fully materialised evidence node that can be inserted into the EvidenceGraph.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Evidence {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub observation: Observation,
    pub kind: EvidenceKind,
    pub provenance: ProvenanceLevel,
    pub belief_delta: BeliefVector,
    pub timestamp: u64,
    pub related_strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
}

impl Evidence {
    pub fn from_observation(
        obs: Observation,
        kind: EvidenceKind,
        belief_delta: BeliefVector,
        related_strategy_id: Option<Uuid>,
    ) -> Self {
        let timestamp = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();

        let provenance = if obs.raw.contains("[READ_OK]") || obs.raw.contains("[CMD_OK]") {
            ProvenanceLevel::Observed { byte_count: obs.raw.len() }
        } else {
            ProvenanceLevel::Hypothesized { rationale: "Derived from executor stdout".to_string() }
        };

        Self {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            observation: obs,
            kind,
            provenance,
            belief_delta,
            timestamp,
            related_strategy_id,
        }
    }

    /// Heuristic classifier with explicit PolicyDenial extraction.
    pub fn classify(raw: &str, success: bool) -> (EvidenceKind, Option<ExecutionFailureKind>) {
        let lower = raw.to_lowercase();

        if lower.contains("sandbox_violation")
            || lower.contains("path_traversal")
            || lower.contains("threat pattern")
        {
            let path = raw.split(':').nth(1).unwrap_or("unknown_path").trim().to_string();
            return (
                EvidenceKind::SecurityViolation,
                Some(ExecutionFailureKind::PolicyDenial {
                    path,
                    reason: "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED".to_string(),
                }),
            );
        }

        if lower.contains("no such file or directory") || lower.contains("os error 2") {
            return (
                EvidenceKind::CommandFailure,
                Some(ExecutionFailureKind::ResourceNotFound { target: raw.to_string() }),
            );
        }

        if lower.contains("pattern_not_found")
            || lower.contains("function_not_found")
            || lower.contains("action_failed")
            || lower.contains("[action failed]")
        {
            return (
                EvidenceKind::CommandFailure,
                Some(ExecutionFailureKind::ExecutionError {
                    exit_code: 1,
                    stderr: raw.to_string(),
                }),
            );
        }

        if lower.contains("[cmd_ok]") || lower.contains("[read_ok]") || lower.contains("[write_ok]")
        {
            return if success {
                (EvidenceKind::CommandSuccess, None)
            } else {
                (EvidenceKind::CommandFailure, None)
            };
        }

        if lower.contains("todo") || lower.contains("placeholder") {
            return (EvidenceKind::ArchitecturalSmell, None);
        }

        if success {
            (EvidenceKind::PositiveSignal, None)
        } else {
            (EvidenceKind::Neutral, None)
        }
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;

pub struct LightVerifier;

impl LightVerifier {
    pub async fn quick_check() -> Result<String> {
        let out = tokio::process::Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["check", "--quiet", "--message-format=short"])
            .output()
            .await?;
        Ok(if out.status.success() { "[VERIFY_OK]".into() } else { "[VERIFY_FAIL]".into() })
    }

/// Calculates the nth Fibonacci number using checked arithmetic to prevent overflow.
/// Returns None if the result exceeds the capacity of u128.
pub fn fibonacci(n: u32) -> Option<u128> {
    if n == 0 {
        return Some(0);
    }
    if n == 1 {
        return Some(1);
    }

    let mut a: u128 = 0;
    let mut b: u128 = 1;

    for _ in 2..=n {
        let next = a.checked_add(b)?;
        a = b;
        b = next;
    }

    Some(b)
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/observation_adapter.rs

use nexus_executive_cortex::schema::{Observation, ObservationEnvelope};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// Translates raw, ad-hoc runtime events into pure, timestamped Observations.
pub struct ObservationAdapter;

impl ObservationAdapter {
    /// Core wrapping function to ensure all observations are uniformly timestamped and ID'd.
    pub fn wrap(observation: Observation) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        let timestamp = SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs();

        ObservationEnvelope {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            timestamp,
            observation,
        }
    }

    /// Translation: User inputs a directive via CLI or Web UI
    pub fn from_human_directive(directive: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::HumanDirective {
            directive: directive.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: The LLM planner exhausts its retry quota
    pub fn from_planner_timeout() -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::PlannerTimeout)
    }

    /// Translation: A MicroAgent finishes its assigned task
    pub fn from_task_completion(task_id: &str, output: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::TaskCompleted {
            task_id: task_id.to_string(),
            output: output.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: An agent hits a fatal error
    pub fn from_goal_failure(goal_id: Uuid, reason: &str) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::GoalFailed {
            goal_id,
            reason: reason.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// Translation: Filtered health spikes from the thermal/system monitor
    pub fn from_health_spike(cpu: f32, ram: f32) -> ObservationEnvelope {
        Self::wrap(Observation::HealthChanged { cpu, ram })
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum DirectiveClass {
    ShellOp,
    TargetedEdit { file_hint: String },
    RepoDoctor,
    SecurityAudit,
    WasmForge,
    Inquiry,
    FeatureBuild,
    General,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ContextBudget {
    pub max_file_chars: usize,
    pub max_obs_lines: usize,
    pub max_schema_chars: usize,
    pub max_output_tokens: u32,
}

impl ContextBudget {
    pub fn minimal() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 1_500,
            max_obs_lines: 2,
            max_schema_chars: 400,
            max_output_tokens: 1024,
        }
    }
    pub fn standard() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 8_000,
            max_obs_lines: 6,
            max_schema_chars: 1_200,
            max_output_tokens: 4096,
        }
    }
    pub fn doctor() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 15_000,
            max_obs_lines: 10,
            max_schema_chars: 1_500,
            max_output_tokens: 8192,
        }
    }
    pub fn security() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_file_chars: 20_000,
            max_obs_lines: 10,
            max_schema_chars: 2_000,
            max_output_tokens: 8192,
        }
    }
}

pub struct AgentProfile {
    pub class: DirectiveClass,
    pub primary_model: &'static str,
    pub max_turns: u8,
    pub budget: ContextBudget,
    pub persona: String,
}

pub struct IntelligentRouter;
impl IntelligentRouter {
    pub fn classify(d: &str) -> DirectiveClass {
        let d = d.to_lowercase();
        if ["diagnose", "doctor", "fix repo", "suture"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor;
        }
        if ["cargo check", "ls ", "find "].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::ShellOp;
        }
        if ["fix", "patch", "edit"].iter().any(|k| d.contains(k)) {
            return DirectiveClass::TargetedEdit { file_hint: "target".into() };
        }
        DirectiveClass::General
    }

    pub fn route(directive: &str) -> AgentProfile {
        let class = Self::classify(directive);
        let persona = r#"IDENTITY: NEXUS Sovereign Agent.
STRICT SYSTEM RULE: You MUST output EXACTLY ONE valid JSON array of AgentAction objects. No markdown.

MANDATORY SCHEMA:
[
  { "type": "patch_function", "path": "...", "function_name": "...", "new_body": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "diff_patch", "path": "...", "search": "...", "replace": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "write_file", "path": "...", "content": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "read_file", "path": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "execute_command", "command": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "search_memory", "query": "...", "reasoning": "..." },
  { "type": "halt", "reasoning": "..." }
]

CRITICAL RULES:
1. NEVER use `write_file` to modify an existing function. You MUST use `patch_function` or `diff_patch`.
2. `patch_function` requires `path`, `function_name`, and the full replacement code in `new_body`.
3. `diff_patch` requires `search` (the exact old string) and `replace` (the exact new string). DO NOT output unified diffs or use a `diff` field.
4. When you have achieved the task objective, include the `halt` action in your JSON array.
5. You MUST escape all newlines inside JSON strings as `\n` (e.g. "line1\nline2").
6. CRITICAL HALT OVERRIDE: You are strictly forbidden from issuing a [HALT] command if your objective requires code modification (patching). Finding the target code via 'grep' or 'cat' is only reconnaissance. You MUST verify the presence of a successful [PATCH_FUNCTION_OK], [WRITE_OK], or [DIFF_PATCH_OK] in your physical evidence buffer before you are authorized to halt. Halting before patching is considered a critical mission failure."#;

        let (primary_model, budget) = match class {
            DirectiveClass::RepoDoctor | DirectiveClass::SecurityAudit => {
                ("gemma-4-31b-it", ContextBudget::doctor())
            }
            _ => ("gemini-3.1-flash-lite", ContextBudget::standard()),
        };

        AgentProfile { class, primary_model, max_turns: 8, budget, persona: persona.to_string() }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/wasm_runtime.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use wasmtime::*;

pub struct WasmRuntime;

impl WasmRuntime {
    fn make_engine() -> Result<Engine> {
        let mut config = Config::new();
        // Size + speed balance for ARM64 / low-RAM nodes
        config.cranelift_opt_level(OptLevel::SpeedAndSize);
        // Hard limits so sidecars cannot starve the cortex
        config.consume_fuel(true);
        config.epoch_interruption(true);
        // Cheaper instantiation
        config.memory_init_cow(true);
        // Avoid memory spikes on constrained devices
        config.parallel_compilation(false);
        Ok(Engine::new(&config)?)
    }

    pub fn run_bytes(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, input: f32) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module = Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes)?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])?;
        let func = instance.get_typed_func::<f32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)?;
        Ok(func.call(&mut store, input)?)
    }

    pub fn run_with_buffer(bytes: &[u8], func_name: &str, data: &[u8]) -> Result<f32> {
        let engine = Self::make_engine()?;
        let module =
            Module::from_binary(&engine, bytes).map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_LOAD_FAULT: {}", e))?;
        let mut store = Store::new(&engine, ());
        store.set_fuel(50_000_000)?;
        let instance = Instance::new(&mut store, &module, &[])
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_INST_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let memory = instance
            .get_memory(&mut store, "memory")
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("WASM module must export memory"))?;

        let get_ptr = instance
            .get_typed_func::<(), i32>(&mut store, "get_buffer_ptr")
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Missing get_buffer_ptr export: {}", e))?;
        let ptr = get_ptr.call(&mut store, ())? as usize;

        if data.len() > 65_536 {
            return Err(anyhow!("VMO payload exceeds 64KB sandbox."));
        }

        // WASMtime 43+ API: Use .write() directly on the memory slice
        memory
            .write(&mut store, ptr, data)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_MEMORY_WRITE_FAULT: {}", e))?;

        let func = instance
            .get_typed_func::<i32, f32>(&mut store, func_name)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("WASM_FUNC_MISSING: {}", e))?;

        Ok(func.call(&mut store, data.len() as i32)?)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cognition_graph.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum NodeKind {
    Observation,
    Verification,
    Reflection,
    Hypothesis,
    ProposedAction { action_json: String },
    Strategy,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct VerificationReport {
    pub goal_satisfied: bool,
    pub evidence_confidence: f32,
    pub independent_check_passed: bool,
    pub rationale: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveNode {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub kind: NodeKind,
    pub content: String,
    pub belief_score: f32,
    pub computation_cost: u32,
    pub is_active: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum EdgeRelation {
    Supports,
    Contradicts,
    Prerequisite,
    DerivesFrom,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GraphEdge {
    pub source: Uuid,
    pub target: Uuid,
    pub relation: EdgeRelation,
    pub weight: f32,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct CognitiveGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<Uuid, CognitiveNode>,
    pub edges: Vec<GraphEdge>,
    pub frontiers: HashSet<Uuid>,
}

impl CognitiveGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn add_node(
        &mut self,
        kind: NodeKind,
        content: &str,
        initial_belief: f32,
        cost: u32,
    ) -> Uuid {
        let id = Uuid::new_v4();
        let node = CognitiveNode {
            id,
            kind,
            content: content.to_string(),
            belief_score: initial_belief,
            computation_cost: cost,
            is_active: false,
        };
        self.nodes.insert(id, node);
        self.frontiers.insert(id);
        id
    }

    pub fn add_edge(&mut self, source: Uuid, target: Uuid, relation: EdgeRelation, weight: f32) {
        self.edges.push(GraphEdge { source, target, relation, weight });
    }

    pub fn propagate_beliefs(&mut self) {
        // Accumulate signed impacts first so multiple edges targeting the same
        // hypothesis are combined instead of overwriting each other.
        let mut delta: HashMap<Uuid, f32> = HashMap::new();
        const DAMPING: f32 = 0.85;

        for edge in &self.edges {
            let Some(source_node) = self.nodes.get(&edge.source) else {
                continue;
            };
            let impact = source_node.belief_score * edge.weight;
            let signed = match edge.relation {
                EdgeRelation::Supports => impact,
                EdgeRelation::Contradicts => -impact,
                // Prerequisite / DerivesFrom intentionally ignored for score updates
                _ => continue,
            };
            *delta.entry(edge.target).or_insert(0.0) += signed;
        }

        for (id, d) in delta {
            if let Some(node) = self.nodes.get_mut(&id) {
                let candidate = (node.belief_score + d).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
                // Keep a fraction of the previous score (PageRank-style damping)
                node.belief_score = DAMPING * candidate + (1.0 - DAMPING) * node.belief_score;
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn select_best_hypothesis(&self) -> Option<&CognitiveNode> {
        self.nodes
            .values()
            // ⚡ FIX: Filter out dead hypotheses (belief < 0.15).
            // v1.9: When BeliefVector is available on the node, prefer may_prove() over raw score.
            .filter(|n| matches!(n.kind, NodeKind::Hypothesis) && n.belief_score >= 0.15)
            .max_by(|a, b| {
                let score_a = a.belief_score / (a.computation_cost as f32).max(1.0);
                let score_b = b.belief_score / (b.computation_cost as f32).max(1.0);
                score_a.partial_cmp(&score_b).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal)
            })
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/conductor.rs 
=========================================
use crate::metrics::METRICS;
use crate::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use crate::router::AgentProfile;
use nexus_model_hub::factory::AgentFactory;
use nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::ContextPacket;
use nexus_substrate::token_ledger::wait_for_quota;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const ESTIMATED_TOKENS: u32 = 2_000;

pub struct Conductor;

pub struct TurnContext<'a> {
    pub orch: &'a Orchestrator,
    pub client: &'a Client,
    pub api_key: &'a str,
    pub tx: Option<broadcast::Sender<String>>,
}

impl Conductor {
    pub async fn run_turn(
        profile: &AgentProfile,
        prompt: String,
        turn: usize,
        ctx: TurnContext<'_>,
        errors: usize,
    ) -> Result<String, String> {
        METRICS.api_calls.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

        let model = AgentFactory::get_model_for_turn(turn, errors);
        let _ = wait_for_quota(model, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

        let neural_ctx = ContextPacket {
            system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
            model_override: Some(model.to_string()),
            ..Default::default()
        };

        if let Some(ref sender) = ctx.tx {
            let _ = sender.send(
                json!({"type": "NEURAL_THOUGHT", "payload": format!("[CONDUCTOR] turn {} → {}", turn, model)}).to_string()
            );
        }

        let result =
            ctx.orch.run(prompt.clone(), neural_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx.clone()).await;

        if let Err(ref e) = result {
            if e.contains("STALE")
                || e.contains("500")
                || e.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED")
                || e.contains("INTERNAL")
                || e.contains("CONTEXT_OVERFLOW")
            {
                METRICS.task_failures.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);

                let failover = AgentFactory::failover_model(model);
                tracing::warn!("[CONDUCTOR] {} failed → failing over to {}", model, failover);
                let _ = wait_for_quota(failover, ESTIMATED_TOKENS).await;

                let fail_ctx = ContextPacket {
                    system_prompt: Some(profile.persona.clone()),
                    model_override: Some(failover.to_string()),
                    ..Default::default()
                };
                return ctx.orch.run(prompt, fail_ctx, ctx.client, ctx.api_key, ctx.tx).await;
            }
        }
        result
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/motor_cortex.rs 
=========================================
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::intent_contract::IntentContract;
use crate::policy::Capability;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum MotorAction {
    ExecuteCommand { command: String },
    WriteFile { path: String, content: String },
    ReadFile { path: String },
    ReadFileChunk { path: String, start_line: usize, max_lines: usize },
    SearchCode { path: String, query: String },
    SearchMemory { query: String },
    DiffPatch { path: String, search: String, replace: String },
    PatchFunction { path: String, function_name: String, new_body: String },
    Halt { reasoning: String },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct MotorCommand {
    pub command_id: Uuid,
    pub intent_id: Uuid,
    pub goal_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub action: MotorAction,
    pub capability: Capability,
    pub expected_outcome: String,
    pub created_at: u64,
}

pub struct MotorCortex;

impl MotorCortex {
    pub fn translate(
        intent: &IntentContract,
        goal_id: Option<Uuid>,
        action: AgentAction,
        capability: Capability,
        expected_outcome: String,
    ) -> MotorCommand {
        let motor_action = match action {
            AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, .. } => MotorAction::ExecuteCommand { command },
            AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, .. } => MotorAction::WriteFile { path, content },
            AgentAction::ReadFile { path, .. } => MotorAction::ReadFile { path },
            AgentAction::ReadFileChunk { path, start_line, max_lines, .. } => MotorAction::ReadFileChunk { path, start_line, max_lines },
            AgentAction::SearchCode { path, query, .. } => MotorAction::SearchCode { path, query },
            AgentAction::SearchMemory { query, .. } => MotorAction::SearchMemory { query },
            AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace, .. } => MotorAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace },
            AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body, .. } => MotorAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body },
            AgentAction::Halt { reasoning } => MotorAction::Halt { reasoning },
            _ => MotorAction::Halt { reasoning: "Fallback halt for unmapped action".to_string() },
        };

        let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .unwrap_or_default()
            .as_secs();

        MotorCommand {
            command_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
            intent_id: intent.id,
            goal_id,
            action: motor_action,
            capability,
            expected_outcome,
            created_at: now,
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/evolution_engine.rs 
=========================================
use crate::fitness_evaluator::FitnessEvaluator;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::wasm_forge::WasmForge;
use anyhow::Result;
use base64::{engine::general_purpose, Engine as _};
use nexus_substrate::vector_bridge;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct EvolutionEngine;

impl EvolutionEngine {
    pub async fn evolve_and_memorize(
        _client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        objective: &str,
        candidate_code: &str,
        test_payload: &[u8],
        func_name: &str,
    ) -> Result<f32> {
        tracing::info!(
            "\x1b[35m>> [EVOLUTION] Synthesizing candidate genome for:\x1b[0m {}",
            objective
        );

        // 1. Synthesize the candidate code into a WASM cell
        let wasm_b64 = WasmForge::synthesize_cell(candidate_code).await?;
        let wasm_bytes = general_purpose::STANDARD.decode(&wasm_b64)?;

        tracing::info!("\x1b[36m>> [EVOLUTION] Candidate compiled. Initiating physical fitness benchmark...\x1b[0m");

        // 2. Evaluate physical fitness (Speed, Memory, Thermal)
        let report = FitnessEvaluator::evaluate_wasm_cell(&wasm_bytes, func_name, test_payload)?;

        tracing::info!(
            "\x1b[33m>> [EVOLUTION] Composite Fitness Score: {:.2}\x1b[0m ({}ms latency, {:.2}KB mem, {:.2}C temp delta)",
            report.composite_score, report.execution_time_ms, report.memory_delta_kb, report.thermal_impact
        );

        // 3. Selection: Only memorize if it meets a high fitness threshold
        if report.composite_score > 100.0 {
            tracing::info!("\x1b[1;32m>> [EVOLUTION] High-Fitness Pattern Detected! Extracting semantic embedding...\x1b[0m");

            // Embed the context
            let embedding_text = format!("OBJECTIVE: {}\nCODE:\n{}", objective, candidate_code);
            let embedding = vector_bridge::get_embedding(api_key, &embedding_text).await.ok();

            let insight = format!(
                "[HIGH_FITNESS_PATTERN] Score: {:.2} | Algorithm successfully optimized for ARM64 constraints. \nCODE_SNIPPET:\n{}",
                report.composite_score,
                &candidate_code[..candidate_code.len().min(500)]
            );

            // Store in Memory Palace
            let mem = memory;
            mem.store_memory("EvolutionEngine", objective, &insight, embedding).await?;

            tracing::info!("\x1b[1;36m>> [EVOLUTION] Genetic material successfully committed to VectorVault.\x1b[0m");
        } else {
            tracing::warn!("\x1b[90m>> [EVOLUTION] Candidate failed to meet fitness threshold. Discarding genome.\x1b[0m");
        }

        Ok(report.composite_score)
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use nexus_executive_cortex::{DecisionSink, schema::{Decision, ExecutiveIntent}};

/// Translates pure Executive Decisions into concrete physical side-effects.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct RuntimeDispatcher;

impl RuntimeDispatcher {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }
}

impl DecisionSink for RuntimeDispatcher {
    fn dispatch(&self, decision: Decision) -> Result<()> {
        // Log the pure decision intent before applying physical side-effects
        println!(
            "\x1b[35m>> [DISPATCHER] Policy evaluating intent: {:?} | Confidence: {:.2}\x1b[0m",
            decision.intent,
            decision.confidence
        );
        println!("\x1b[90m   Reason: {}\x1b[0m", decision.reason);
        
        // The Policy Routing Table
        match decision.intent {
            ExecutiveIntent::RequestPlanning => {
                println!("\x1b[32m   -> Action: Spawning DAG Planner for new directive.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: Orchestrator DAG invocation will go here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::CompleteGoal => {
                println!("\x1b[32m   -> Action: Archiving Goal to Memory Cortex and updating Telemetry.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: MemoryActor & Telemetry sync goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Escalate => {
                println!("\x1b[33m   -> Action: System health critical! Escalating to Human Operator.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: UI WebSocket alert goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Abort => {
                println!("\x1b[31m   -> Action: Irrecoverable failure. Safely freezing the DAG.\x1b[0m");
                // Integration Point: Graceful soft-halt mechanism goes here.
            }
            ExecutiveIntent::Continue => {
                println!("\x1b[36m   -> Action: No execution required. Proceeding nominally.\x1b[0m");
            }
            _ => {
                println!("\x1b[90m   -> Action: Policy for {:?} is stubbed for future implementation.\x1b[0m", decision.intent);
            }
        }
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::observation_adapter::ObservationAdapter;
    use nexus_executive_cortex::ExecutiveCortex;

    #[test]
    fn test_pure_cortex_pipeline() {
        println!("\n\x1b[1m--- INITIATING CORTEX PIPELINE SIMULATION ---\x1b[0m");

        // 1. Boot the pure state machine and the side-effect dispatcher
        let mut cortex = ExecutiveCortex::new();
        let dispatcher = RuntimeDispatcher::new();

        // 2. The Real World happens (A user types a command)
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 1] Real world event: User typed a directive.\x1b[0m");
        let envelope = ObservationAdapter::from_human_directive("Refactor the memory bus architecture.");
        
        // 3. The Cortex evaluates the observation (Zero side-effects)
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 2] Cortex processing Observation Envelope (ID: {})...\x1b[0m", envelope.id);
        let decision = cortex.receive(envelope);

        // 4. The Dispatcher physically acts on the pure decision
        println!("\x1b[90m[Step 3] Routing Decision to Dispatcher...\x1b[0m");
        dispatcher.dispatch(decision).unwrap();
        
        println!("\x1b[1m--- SIMULATION COMPLETE ---\x1b[0m\n");
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/memory_consolidation.rs 
=========================================
use crate::memory_types::{CognitiveMemory, MemoryKind};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

pub struct MemoryConsolidator;

impl MemoryConsolidator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn calculate_retention(memory: &CognitiveMemory) -> f32 {
        let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap().as_secs() as i64;

        let age = (now - memory.created_at).max(0) as f32;

        let decay = (-age / 86400.0).exp();

        let score = memory.importance * memory.confidence * decay;

        score.clamp(0.0, 1.0)
    }

    pub fn should_consolidate(memory: &CognitiveMemory) -> bool {
        match memory.kind {
            MemoryKind::Failure | MemoryKind::Reflection | MemoryKind::Goal => true,

            _ => Self::calculate_retention(memory) > 0.5,
        }
    }
}

impl Default for MemoryConsolidator {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

use crate::memory_types::{CognitiveMemory, MemoryKind};

use uuid::Uuid;

pub struct ReflectionEngine;

impl ReflectionEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn reflect(&self, memories: &[CognitiveMemory]) -> Option<CognitiveMemory> {
        if memories.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }

        let mut lesson = String::from("Reflection summary:\n");

        for memory in memories.iter().take(5) {
            lesson.push_str(&format!("- {}\n", memory.content));
        }

        Some(CognitiveMemory {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("reflection_{}", Uuid::new_v4()),

            content: lesson,

            source: "ReflectionEngine".into(),

            confidence: 0.7,

            importance: 0.8,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec!["reflection".into(), "lesson".into()],
        })
    }
}

impl Default for ReflectionEngine {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

pub fn classify_lesson(lesson: &str) -> crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory {
    let category = if lesson.contains("RAM") || lesson.contains("memory") {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::HardwareConstraint
    } else if lesson.contains("failed") {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::FailurePattern
    } else {
        crate::memory_semantic::SemanticCategory::GeneralKnowledge
    };

    crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory::new(category, lesson, 0.8)
}

use crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory;

    pub limit: usize,

    pub confidence: f32,
}

pub struct MemoryRecallEngine;

impl MemoryRecallEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    pub fn rank(&self, memories: Vec<CognitiveMemory>, _context: &str) -> RecallResult {
        let count = memories.len();

        RecallResult { memories, confidence: if count > 0 { 0.8 } else { 0.0 } }
    }
}

        Self::new()
    }

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_engine.rs 
=========================================
use crate::evidence::BeliefVector;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefState {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub proposition: String,
    pub confidence: f32,
    pub evidence_count: usize,
    pub stability: f32,
    /// Rich multidimensional view (canonical path going forward).
    pub vector: BeliefVector,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefEvidence {
    pub source: String,
    pub observation: String,
    pub reliability: f32,
}

pub struct BeliefEngine;

impl BeliefEngine {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self
    }

    /// Compatibility entry point – routes through BeliefVector::bayesian_update.
    pub fn update(&self, current: Option<BeliefState>, evidence: BeliefEvidence) -> BeliefState {
        let likelihood = evidence.reliability.clamp(0.0, 1.0);
        match current {
            Some(mut belief) => {
                // Canonical update
                belief.vector = belief.vector.bayesian_update(likelihood, 0.85);
                // Keep scalar fields in sync for legacy callers
                belief.confidence = belief.vector.scalar_score();
                belief.evidence_count += 1;
                belief.stability = (belief.stability + 0.05).min(1.0);
                belief
            }
            None => {
                let vector = BeliefVector::default().bayesian_update(likelihood, 0.85);
                BeliefState {
                    id: Uuid::new_v4(),
                    proposition: evidence.observation,
                    confidence: vector.scalar_score(),
                    evidence_count: 1,
                    stability: 0.5,
                    vector,
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

impl Default for BeliefEngine {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl BeliefState {
    pub fn update_confidence(&mut self, delta: f32) {
        self.confidence = (self.confidence + delta).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    }

    pub fn apply_delta(&mut self, delta: crate::belief_delta::BeliefDelta) {
        self.confidence = delta.new_confidence;
        // Keep vector roughly in sync
        self.vector.implementation_confidence = delta.new_confidence;
    }

    /// Hard gate – prefer this over raw confidence checks.
    pub fn may_prove(&self, required_artifacts: u8, threshold: f32) -> bool {
        self.vector.may_prove(required_artifacts, threshold)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/goal_adaptation.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum GoalAdaptationAction {
    ContinueGoal,

    ModifyStrategy,

    PauseGoal,

    TerminateGoal,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct GoalAdaptationDecision {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub goal_id: Uuid,

    pub action: GoalAdaptationAction,

    pub reason: String,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct GoalAdaptationEngine;

impl GoalAdaptationEngine {
    pub fn from_belief_action(
        goal_id: Uuid,
        action: crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction,
    ) -> GoalAdaptationDecision {
        let (adaptation, reason) = match action {
            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Continue => {
                (GoalAdaptationAction::ContinueGoal, "Belief confidence supports continuation")
            }

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Adapt => (
                GoalAdaptationAction::ModifyStrategy,
                "Belief degradation requires strategy adaptation",
            ),

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Suspend => (
                GoalAdaptationAction::PauseGoal,
                "Belief confidence insufficient, pausing objective",
            ),

            crate::belief_policy::BeliefAction::Abandon => {
                (GoalAdaptationAction::TerminateGoal, "Evidence no longer supports objective")
            }
        };

        GoalAdaptationDecision {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            goal_id,

            action: adaptation,

            reason: reason.to_string(),

            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/outcome_verifier.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct OutcomeAssessment {
    pub success_probability: f32,
    pub deviation_score: f32,
    pub confidence_delta: f32,
    pub lessons: Vec<String>,
}

pub struct OutcomeVerifier;

impl OutcomeVerifier {
    pub fn evaluate(expected: &str, observed: &str) -> OutcomeAssessment {
        let success = if expected == observed { 1.0 } else { 0.5 };

        OutcomeAssessment {
            success_probability: success,
            deviation_score: 1.0 - success,
            confidence_delta: success - 0.5,
            lessons: vec![format!("Expected: {} Observed: {}", expected, observed)],
        }
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/strategy_revision.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum StrategyDecision {
    Continue,
    Modify,
    Escalate,
    Abandon,
}

pub struct StrategyRevision;

impl StrategyRevision {
    pub fn decide(confidence: f32) -> StrategyDecision {
        if confidence > 0.75 {
            StrategyDecision::Continue
        } else if confidence > 0.45 {
            StrategyDecision::Modify
        } else if confidence > 0.20 {
            StrategyDecision::Escalate
        } else {
            StrategyDecision::Abandon
        }
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cognition_loop.rs 
=========================================
use std::sync::Arc;

use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::outcome_verifier::OutcomeVerifier;
use crate::strategy_revision::StrategyRevision;

pub struct CognitiveLoop {
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
}

impl CognitiveLoop {
    pub fn new(memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>) -> Self {
        Self { memory }
    }

    pub async fn evaluate_cycle(&self, expected: &str, observed: &str) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let assessment = OutcomeVerifier::evaluate(expected, observed);

        let decision = StrategyRevision::decide(assessment.success_probability);

        self.memory
            .remember(crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
                id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

                kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

                key: "cognitive_loop_result".into(),

                content: format!(
                    "Decision {:?}, confidence {}",
                    decision, assessment.success_probability
                ),

                source: "CognitiveLoop".into(),

                confidence: assessment.success_probability,

                importance: 0.8,

                created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

                tags: vec!["belief".into(), "feedback".into()],
            })
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_delta.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefDelta {
    pub previous_confidence: f32,

    pub evidence_strength: f32,

    pub failure_penalty: f32,

    pub reflection_gain: f32,

    pub new_confidence: f32,

    pub explanation: String,
}

impl BeliefDelta {
    pub fn calculate(
        previous: f32,
        evidence: f32,
        failure: f32,
        reflection: f32,
        explanation: String,
    ) -> Self {
        let updated = (previous + evidence - failure + reflection).clamp(0.0, 1.0);

        Self {
            previous_confidence: previous,
            evidence_strength: evidence,
            failure_penalty: failure,
            reflection_gain: reflection,
            new_confidence: updated,
            explanation,
        }
    }
}

            delta.previous_confidence,
            delta.new_confidence,
            delta.evidence_strength,
            delta.failure_penalty,
            delta.reflection_gain,
            delta.explanation
        )
    }
}

        )
    }

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_validation.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefValidationEvent {
    pub prediction: String,

    pub observed: String,

    pub error_score: f32,

    pub confidence_delta: f32,

    pub lesson: String,
}

impl BeliefValidationEvent {
    pub fn new(prediction: String, observed: String) -> Self {
        let error_score = if prediction == observed { 0.0 } else { 1.0 };

        let confidence_delta = if error_score == 0.0 { 0.05 } else { -0.25 };

        let lesson = if error_score == 0.0 {
            "Prediction confirmed".to_string()
        } else {
            format!("Prediction failed. Expected '{}', observed '{}'", prediction, observed)
        };

        Self { prediction, observed, error_score, confidence_delta, lesson }
    }
}

pub struct BeliefValidationEngine;
impl BeliefValidationEngine {
    pub fn validate(prediction: String, observed: String) -> BeliefValidationEvent {
        BeliefValidationEvent::new(prediction, observed)
    }
}

    pub target_changed: bool,

    pub unrelated_files_unchanged: bool,

    pub syntax_valid: bool,

    pub tests_passed: bool,

    pub memory_recorded: bool,
}

impl MutationContract {
    pub fn verified(&self) -> bool {
        self.target_changed
            && self.unrelated_files_unchanged
            && self.syntax_valid
            && self.tests_passed
            && self.memory_recorded
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/mutation/governor.rs 
=========================================
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ApprovalDecision {
    Approved(String),
    Rejected(String),
}

pub struct MutationGovernor;

impl MutationGovernor {
    pub fn evaluate(
        files_changed: usize,
        tests_passed: bool,
        backup_exists: bool,
    ) -> ApprovalDecision {
        if files_changed > 1 {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("mutation scope exceeds policy".into());
        }

        if !tests_passed {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("verification failed".into());
        }

        if !backup_exists {
            return ApprovalDecision::Rejected("rollback unavailable".into());
        }

        ApprovalDecision::Approved("mutation satisfies safety policy".into())
    }
}

    pub action: String,

    pub nodes: Vec<BuildNode>,
}

    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

        Self { nodes: Vec::new() }

use std::collections::HashMap;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct NodeId(pub String);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Node {
    pub id: NodeId,
    pub label: String,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Edge {
    pub source: NodeId,
    pub target: NodeId,
    pub edge_type: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Default)]
pub struct GraphMetadata {
    pub version: String,
    pub total_nodes: usize,
    pub total_edges: usize,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct DependencyGraph {
    pub nodes: HashMap<NodeId, Node>,
    pub edges: Vec<Edge>,
    pub metadata: GraphMetadata,
}

impl DependencyGraph {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            nodes: HashMap::new(),
            edges: Vec::new(),
            metadata: GraphMetadata::default(),
        }
    }
}

impl Default for DependencyGraph {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/repository_intelligence/symbol_index.rs 
=========================================
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct SymbolId(pub String);

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Span {
    pub start: usize,
    pub end: usize,
    pub line: usize,
}

impl Ord for Span {
    fn cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> std::cmp::Ordering {
        self.line
            .cmp(&other.line)
            .then_with(|| self.start.cmp(&other.start))
            .then_with(|| self.end.cmp(&other.end))
    }
}

impl PartialOrd for Span {
    fn partial_cmp(&self, other: &Self) -> Option<std::cmp::Ordering> {
        Some(self.cmp(other))
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolInfo {
    pub id: SymbolId,
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub span: Span,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolReference {
    pub symbol_id: SymbolId,
    pub span: Span,
    pub file_path: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct SymbolIndex {
    pub symbols: HashMap<String, SymbolInfo>,
    pub references: Vec<SymbolReference>,
    pub span_map: BTreeMap<Span, SymbolId>,
}

impl SymbolIndex {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn insert_symbol(&mut self, info: SymbolInfo) {
        self.span_map.insert(info.span.clone(), info.id.clone());
        self.symbols.insert(info.id.0.clone(), info);
    }

    pub fn add_reference(&mut self, reference: SymbolReference) {
        self.references.push(reference);
    }

    pub fn get_symbol(&self, id: &SymbolId) -> Option<&SymbolInfo> {
        self.symbols.get(&id.0)
    }

    pub fn find_by_span(&self, span: &Span) -> Option<&SymbolId> {
        self.span_map.get(span)
    }
}

use crate::adaptive_mission::assumptions::{Assumption, AssumptionError};
use crate::adaptive_mission::state::MissionState;

/// Universal Validator for evaluating assumptions.
/// Designed to strictly interface with the Gemma 3 API for dynamic validation.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct AssumptionValidator {
    // API client configuration will be seated here (Zero Fragmentation)
}

impl AssumptionValidator {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Evaluates if an assumption holds true given the current mission state.
    pub async fn validate(
        &self,
        assumption: &Assumption,
        _state: &MissionState,
    ) -> Result<(), AssumptionError> {
        // Pre-API structural validation
        if assumption.id.is_empty() {
            return Err(AssumptionError::ValidationFailed(
                "UNKNOWN".to_string(),
                "Assumption ID cannot be empty".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        if assumption.description.is_empty() {
            return Err(AssumptionError::PreconditionFailed(
                "Description required for Gemma 3 API evaluation".to_string(),
            ));
        }

        // TODO: Seat dynamic Gemma 3 API routing here
        
        Ok(())
    }
}

use serde::{Serialize, Deserialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum NodeStatus {
    Pending,
    Success,
    Fracture,
    Vetoed,
}

/// The state payload passed between DAG nodes to prevent Context Amnesia.
/// Injected directly into the Gemma 3 API system prompt.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ContextEnvelope {
    pub previous_node_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub status: NodeStatus,
    pub stderr: Option<String>,          // Captures compiler/OS errors
    pub critic_feedback: Option<String>, // Captures exact veto reasoning
    pub modified_files: Vec<String>,     // Tracks mutations across nodes
}

impl Default for ContextEnvelope {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            previous_node_id: None,
            status: NodeStatus::Pending,
            stderr: None,
            critic_feedback: None,
            modified_files: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/arbiter_agent.rs 
=========================================
use nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct ArbiterAgent {
    pub agent: Arc<MicroAgent>,
}

impl ArbiterAgent {
    pub fn new(agent: Arc<MicroAgent>) -> Self {
        Self { agent }
    }

    pub fn validate_action(action_type: &str, command: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let dangerous_patterns = [
            "rm -rf /",
            "rm -rf ~",
            "mkfs",
            "> /dev/sda",
            "> /dev/nvme",
            "chmod 777 /",
            "/etc/shadow",
            "mv /etc/",
            "mv /usr/",
            ":(){ :|:& };:",
            "dd if=/dev/zero",
            "fork bomb",
            "sudo su",
            "sudo -i",
            "sudo bash",
            "sudo sh",
            "passwd root",
            "chown root",
            "$'\\x",
            "\\u0072\\u006d",
            "base64 -d |",
            "eval $(",
            "exec(",
            "curl | bash",
            "wget | bash",
            "curl | sh",
            "history -c",
            "unset HISTFILE",
        ];

        let normalized = command.to_lowercase();

        // Narrow, explicit exception: allow `rm -rf /tmp/...` for legitimate
        // local temp-folder cleanup without loosening the general root-path
        // rm -rf block (which the broad "rm -rf /" pattern below restores).
        let is_scoped_tmp_cleanup =
            normalized.contains("rm -rf /tmp/") && !normalized.contains("rm -rf /tmp/../");

        for pattern in dangerous_patterns.iter() {
            if normalized.contains(pattern) {
                if *pattern == "rm -rf /" && is_scoped_tmp_cleanup {
                    continue;
                }
                return Err(format!(
                    "SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Threat pattern '{}' detected in {}.",
                    pattern, action_type
                ));
            }
        }

        if normalized.contains("../../../") || normalized.contains("/etc/passwd") {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Directory traversal attempt blocked.".to_string());
        }

        if (normalized.contains("| bash")
            || normalized.contains("| sh")
            || normalized.contains("|bash")
            || normalized.contains("|sh"))
            && !normalized.starts_with("echo")
        {
            return Err("SANDBOX_VIOLATION: Pipe-to-shell pattern blocked.".to_string());
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn evaluate_entropy_delta(base_entropy: f32, new_entropy: f32) -> Result<(), String> {
        let delta = (new_entropy - base_entropy).abs();
        if delta > 0.5 {
            return Err(format!("ENTROPY_ANOMALY: Delta of {:.4} exceeds 0.5 threshold.", delta));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn resolve_consensus(alpha_vector: &[f32], beta_vector: &[f32]) -> bool {
        if alpha_vector.len() != beta_vector.len() {
            return false;
        }
        let diff: f32 =
            alpha_vector.iter().zip(beta_vector.iter()).map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs()).sum();
        diff < 0.5
    }

    pub fn bft_consensus(vectors: &[Vec<f32>], epsilon: f32) -> Option<Vec<f32>> {
        if vectors.is_empty() {
            return None;
        }
        let n = vectors.len();
        let threshold = (2 * n / 3) + 1;
        let dim = vectors[0].len();
        if vectors.iter().any(|v| v.len() != dim) {
            return None;
        }

        let (best_idx, neighbor_count) = (0..n)
            .map(|i| {
                let neighbors = (0..n)
                    .filter(|&j| {
                        vectors[i]
                            .iter()
                            .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                            .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                            .sum::<f32>()
                            < epsilon
                    })
                    .count();
                (i, neighbors)
            })
            .max_by_key(|&(_, count)| count)?;

        if neighbor_count < threshold {
            return None;
        }

        let agreeing: Vec<&Vec<f32>> = (0..n)
            .filter(|&j| {
                vectors[best_idx]
                    .iter()
                    .zip(vectors[j].iter())
                    .map(|(a, b)| (a - b).abs())
                    .sum::<f32>()
                    < epsilon
            })
            .map(|j| &vectors[j])
            .collect();

        let centroid: Vec<f32> = (0..dim)
            .map(|k| agreeing.iter().map(|v| v[k]).sum::<f32>() / agreeing.len() as f32)
            .collect();

        Some(centroid)
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/ast_editor.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::fs;
use tree_sitter::{Parser, Query, QueryCursor};

pub struct AstEditor;

impl AstEditor {
    /// update_function: Finds a function by name and replaces its entire body.
    /// This is the "Semantic Precision" upgrade to replace simple string diffs.
    pub fn read_function(path: &str, function_name: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into())?;

        let tree = parser
            .parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;

        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        for m in matches {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 {
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();
                    return Ok(source_code[start..end].to_string());
                }
            }
        }

        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }

    pub fn update_function(path: &str, function_name: &str, new_body: &str) -> Result<String> {
        let source_code = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut parser = Parser::new();
        parser.set_language(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into())?;

        let tree = parser
            .parse(&source_code, None)
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("AST_PARSE_FAIL: Could not parse {}", path))?;

        // Query to find the specific function node
        let query_str = format!(
            "(function_item name: (identifier) @name (#eq? @name \"{}\")) @func",
            function_name
        );
        let query = Query::new(&tree_sitter_rust::LANGUAGE.into(), &query_str)?;
        let mut cursor = QueryCursor::new();
        let matches = cursor.matches(&query, tree.root_node(), source_code.as_bytes());
        for m in matches {
            for capture in m.captures {
                if capture.index == 1 {
                    // The @func capture
                    let node = capture.node;
                    let start = node.start_byte();
                    let end = node.end_byte();

                    let mut new_code = source_code.clone();
                    new_code.replace_range(start..end, new_body);
                    return Ok(new_code);
                }
            }
        }

        Err(anyhow!("FUNCTION_NOT_FOUND: Could not find function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/belief_policy.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum BeliefAction {
    Continue,
    Adapt,
    Suspend,
    Abandon,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BeliefDecision {
    pub id: Uuid,

    pub belief_id: Uuid,

    pub confidence: f32,

    pub action: BeliefAction,

    pub reason: String,

    pub timestamp: i64,
}

pub struct BeliefDecisionPolicy;

impl BeliefDecisionPolicy {
    pub fn evaluate(belief_id: Uuid, confidence: f32) -> BeliefDecision {
        let (action, reason) = if confidence >= 0.75 {
            (BeliefAction::Continue, "Confidence sufficient to continue objective".to_string())
        } else if confidence >= 0.45 {
            (
                BeliefAction::Adapt,
                "Confidence degraded, strategy adjustment recommended".to_string(),
            )
        } else if confidence >= 0.20 {
            (BeliefAction::Suspend, "Confidence too low, suspend objective".to_string())
        } else {
            (BeliefAction::Abandon, "Evidence no longer supports objective".to_string())
        };

        Self::decision(belief_id, confidence, action, reason)
    }

    fn decision(
        belief_id: Uuid,
        confidence: f32,
        action: BeliefAction,
        reason: String,
    ) -> BeliefDecision {
        BeliefDecision {
            id: Uuid::new_v4(),

            belief_id,

            confidence,

            action,

            reason,

            timestamp: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),
        }
    }
}
