FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/epistemic_engine.rs 
=========================================
#![allow(warnings)]
use crate::json_parser::sanitize_json_escapes;
use crate::model_config::{get_heavy_models, get_light_models};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use tokio::time::Duration;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CriticResponse {
    pub critique: String,
    pub confidence_modifier: f32,
}

pub struct EpistemicEngine;

impl EpistemicEngine {
    pub async fn safe_api_call(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        models: &[String],
        payload: &Value,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let mut last_err = anyhow!("No models available in cascade.");
        
        for model_endpoint in models {
            let is_nvidia = model_endpoint.starts_with("nvidia/") || model_endpoint.starts_with("meta/");
            let mut retries = 0;

            loop {
                let (url, req_payload, use_auth) = if is_nvidia {
                    let nv_key = std::env::var("NVIDIA_API_KEY")
                        .unwrap_or_default()
                        .trim()
                        .trim_matches('"')
                        .to_string();
                    let text = payload
                        .pointer("/contents/0/parts/0/text")
                        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                        .unwrap_or("");
                    let p = serde_json::json!({
                        "model": model_endpoint,
                        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": text}],
                        "max_tokens": 4096,
                        "temperature": 0.1
                    });
                    (
                        "https://integrate.api.nvidia.com/v1/chat/completions".to_string(),
                        p,
                        Some(nv_key),
                    )
                } else {
                    let key = if api_key.is_empty() {
                        std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default().trim().to_string()
                    } else {
                        api_key.to_string()
                    };
                    let clean_model = model_endpoint.replace("models/", "");
                    let u = format!(
                        "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/{}:generateContent?key={}",
                        clean_model,
                        key
                    );
                    (u, payload.clone(), None)
                };

                let mut req = client.post(&url).json(&req_payload);
                if let Some(auth_key) = use_auth {
                    req = req.header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", auth_key));
                }

                // ⚡ NETWORK RETRY LOOP
                let res = match req.send().await {
                    Ok(r) => r,
                    Err(e) => {
                        last_err = anyhow!("Network Error on {}: {}", model_endpoint, e);
                        retries += 1;
                        if retries > 2 { break; } // Failover to next model
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                        continue;
                    }
                };

                let body: Value = res.json().await.unwrap_or_default();

                if is_nvidia {
                    // ⚡ NVIDIA ERROR HANDLING & FAILOVER
                    if let Some(err_msg) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                        last_err = anyhow!("NVIDIA API Error: {}", err_msg);
                        if err_msg.contains("ResourceExhausted") || err_msg.contains("limit reached") {
                            crate::model_config::report_rate_limit(model_endpoint);
                            break; // ⚡ INSTANT FAILOVER to Gemini
                        }
                        retries += 1;
                        if retries > 2 { break; }
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3)).await;
                        continue;
                    }
                } else {
                    // ⚡ GEMINI ERROR HANDLING
                    if let Some(err) = body.pointer("/error/message").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) {
                        last_err = anyhow!("Gemini API Error: {}", err);
                        if err.contains("quota")
                            || err.contains("rate limit")
                            || err.contains("RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED")
                            || err.contains("429")
                        {
                            crate::model_config::report_rate_limit(model_endpoint);
                            break; // ⚡ INSTANT FAILOVER
                        }
                        retries += 1;
                        if retries > 2 { break; }
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(3)).await;
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                let mut final_text = String::new();
                if is_nvidia {
                    if let Some(content) =
                        body.pointer("/choices/0/message/content").and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                    {
                        final_text = content.to_string();
                    }
                    if final_text.trim().is_empty() {
                        if let Some(rc) = body
                            .pointer("/choices/0/message/reasoning_content")
                            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                        {
                            final_text = rc.to_string();
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    if let Some(parts) =
                        body.pointer("/candidates/0/content/parts").and_then(|v| v.as_array())
                    {
                        for part in parts {
                            if part.get("thought").and_then(|t| t.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false) {
                                continue;
                            }
                            if let Some(text) = part.get("text").and_then(|t| t.as_str()) {
                                final_text.push_str(text);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                if final_text.trim().is_empty() {
                    retries += 1;
                    if retries > 2 { break; }
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
                    continue;
                }
                
                return Ok(final_text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "").trim().to_string());
            }
        }
        Err(anyhow!("Model Cascade Exhausted. Final error: {}", last_err))
    }

    pub async fn generate_hypotheses(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        problem: &str,
        world_context: &str,
    ) -> Result<Vec<String>> {
        let prompt = format!("You are AthenaFusionX. Analyze this directive.\nDIRECTIVE: {}\nWORLD CONTEXT: {}\nCRITICAL RULE: If the directive explicitly tells you to write or export a file, at least ONE of your strategies MUST be to directly invoke write_file. If the directive tells you to patch, apply fixes, or edit code, at least ONE of your strategies MUST be to directly invoke diff_patch or patch_function. If the directive tells you to patch, apply fixes, or edit code, at least ONE of your strategies MUST be to directly invoke diff_patch or patch_function.\nGenerate 3 distinct, actionable strategies. OUTPUT ONLY A JSON ARRAY OF STRINGS. No markdown.", problem, world_context);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 2048 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(
            client,
            api_key,
            &crate::model_config::get_heavy_models(),
            &payload,
        )
        .await?;
        let clean_text = sanitize_json_escapes(&text);

        if let Ok(hyps) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<String>>(&clean_text) {
            if !hyps.is_empty() {
                return Ok(hyps);
            }
        }

        let lines: Vec<String> = text
            .lines()
            .map(|l| {
                l.trim()
                    .trim_start_matches(|c: char| {
                        c == '-'
                            || c == '*'
                            || c == '•'
                            || c.is_ascii_digit()
                            || c == '.'
                            || c == '>'
                    })
                    .trim()
                    .to_string()
            })
            .filter(|l| !l.is_empty() && l.len() > 5)
            .take(3)
            .collect();

        if !lines.is_empty() {
            Ok(lines)
        } else {
            Ok(vec![format!("Execute directive: {}", problem)])
        }
    }

    pub async fn propose_action(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        mission: &str,
        strategy: &str,
        world_context: &str,
        evidence_so_far: &str,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let prompt = format!("You are the Action Strategist.
AVAILABLE TOOLS & MANIFEST:
- execute_command <cmd>: Run shell commands, git, or cargo.
- search_code <path> <query>: Fast recursive code search for keywords/patterns across all files in a directory or path.
- read_file_chunk <path> <start_line> <max_lines>: PAGINATED READ for source files. Start at line 0, max_lines 500.
- read_file <path>: Basic file read (only for small config files).
- write_file <path> <content>: Write content to a file.
- diff_patch <path> <search> <replace>: Apply a diff patch to a file.
- patch_function <path> <function_name> <new_body>: Patch a Rust function directly.
- search_memory <query>: Query vector vault.
- halt: Finish mission.\nORIGINAL DIRECTIVE: {}\nSTRATEGY: {}\nWORLD CONTEXT: {}\nEVIDENCE GATHERED SO FAR:\n{}\nPropose the exact next step to fulfill the strategy.\nCRITICAL RULE 0: Do NOT propose directory-listing commands if the path is already visible in WORLD CONTEXT.\nCRITICAL RULE 1: If the directive or strategy specifically asks to use a tool (like 'execute_command', 'search_code', or 'read_file_chunk'), you MUST propose that exact tool call.\nCRITICAL RULE 2: DO NOT RE-PROPOSE AN ACTION THAT WAS JUST REJECTED, VETOED, OR ALREADY ATTEMPTED.\nCRITICAL RULE 3: If EVIDENCE GATHERED SO FAR satisfies the directive, propose 'halt'.\nCRITICAL RULE 4 [CODE SEARCH TERMS]: When using search_code on source code, use single code keywords or symbols (e.g., 'matcher', 'regex', 'executor', 'WalkDir', 'fn ') rather than multi-word English sentences like \"file matching\".\nCRITICAL RULE 5 [DIRECT EXPORT]: If the objective asks to write or export a report/file (e.g., /tmp/ripgrep_gap_analysis.md), your proposal MUST be 'write_file <path> <markdown_content>' using the evidence gathered in previous tasks.\nCRITICAL RULE 6 [DIRECTIVE PRIORITY]: If the ORIGINAL DIRECTIVE explicitly forbids a tool, command, or approach (phrases like \"DO NOT use\", \"CRITICAL DIRECTIVE\", \"never\"), you MUST NOT propose that forbidden action, even if it seems like the only available path. If every remaining option is forbidden or unavailable, propose 'halt' and explain the blocker in your reasoning instead of violating the directive.\nOUTPUT ONLY A SINGLE LINE CONTAINING THE EXACT COMMAND OR ACTION.", mission, strategy, world_context, evidence_so_far);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.2, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_light_models(), &payload)
            .await
    }

    pub async fn criticize_proposal(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        mission: &str,
        strategy: &str,
        proposed_action: &str,
        world_context: &str,
        evidence_history: &str,
    ) -> Result<CriticResponse> {
        let prompt = format!("You are the Adversarial Critic.\nORIGINAL DIRECTIVE: {}\nSTRATEGY: {}\nPROPOSED ACTION: {}\nWORLD CONTEXT: {}\nEVIDENCE HISTORY: {}\nEvaluate if this action is safe and logically sound.\nCRITICAL RULE 0 [NATIVE SUBSTRATE TOOLS]: 'execute_command', 'search_code', 'read_file_chunk', 'search_memory', 'read_file', 'write_file', 'diff_patch', 'patch_function' are fully authorized native substrate tools.\nCRITICAL RULE 1 [PRE-AUTHORIZED PATHS]: Any file path explicitly named in ORIGINAL DIRECTIVE, visible in WORLD CONTEXT, or present in workspace directories is PRE-AUTHORIZED and RESOLVED.\nCRITICAL RULE 2 [IDEMPOTENCY & OVERWRITE GUARD]: If PROPOSED ACTION is write_file to a target path that EVIDENCE HISTORY shows was ALREADY written with [WRITE_OK] in an ancestor task, you MUST VETO the proposal (-0.50) with 'VETO: Target path already written by prior task. Do not overwrite existing deliverable.' unless the task explicitly instructs to overwrite or update it.\nCRITICAL RULE 3: CONSTRUCTIVE VETO. If you issue a negative modifier, you MUST state the exact alternative command to try.\nOUTPUT ONLY VALID JSON:\n{{\"critique\": \"<reason>\", \"confidence_modifier\": <float from -0.5 to 0.5>}}", mission, strategy, proposed_action, world_context, evidence_history);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({"contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}], "generationConfig": { "temperature": 0.1, "maxOutputTokens": 1024 }});
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(
            client,
            api_key,
            &crate::model_config::get_light_models(),
            &payload,
        )
        .await?;
        let clean_text = sanitize_json_escapes(&text);
        serde_json::from_str(&clean_text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }

    pub async fn translate_to_action(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        strategy: &str,
        proposed_action: &str,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let prompt = format!(
            "You are the Action Translator.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nPROPOSED ACTION: {}\nTranslate the PROPOSED ACTION into exactly ONE JSON Array using the schema below:\n\
             VALID JSON ACTIONS:\n\
             1. [{{ \"type\": \"execute_command\", \"command\": \"<bash_command>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             2. [{{ \"type\": \"search_memory\", \"query\": \"<search_query>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             3. [{{ \"type\": \"read_file\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             4. [{{ \"type\": \"write_file\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"content\": \"<data>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             5. [{{ \"type\": \"diff_patch\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"search\": \"<search>\", \"replace\": \"<replace>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             6. [{{ \"type\": \"patch_function\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"function_name\": \"<name>\", \"new_body\": \"<body>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             7. [{{ \"type\": \"read_file_chunk\", \"path\": \"<filepath>\", \"start_line\": 0, \"max_lines\": 500, \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             8. [{{ \"type\": \"search_code\", \"path\": \"<filepath_or_dir>\", \"query\": \"<search_pattern>\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             9. [{{ \"type\": \"halt\", \"reasoning\": \"...\" }}]\n\
             Enclose in a JSON array. Do not output anything else.",
            strategy, proposed_action
        );
        let payload = serde_json::json!({
            "contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.0,
                "responseMimeType": "application/json",
                "maxOutputTokens": 4096,
                "responseSchema": {
                    "type": "ARRAY",
                    "items": {
                        "type": "OBJECT",
                        "properties": {
                            "type": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "command": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "path": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "content": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "search": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "replace": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "function_name": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "new_body": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "query": { "type": "STRING" },
                            "start_line": { "type": "INTEGER" },
                            "max_lines": { "type": "INTEGER" },
                            "reasoning": { "type": "STRING" }
                        },
                        "required": ["type", "reasoning"]
                    }
                }
            }
        });
        Self::safe_api_call(client, api_key, &crate::model_config::get_light_models(), &payload)
            .await
    }

    pub async fn evaluate_evidence(
        client: &Client,
        api_key: &str,
        strategy: &str,
        action: &str,
        evidence: &str,
    ) -> Result<CriticResponse> {
        let truncated_evidence: String = evidence.chars().take(8000).collect();
        let prompt = format!("You are the Scientific Evaluator.\nSTRATEGY: {}\nACTION TAKEN: {}\nEVIDENCE GATHERED:\n{}\nHALT RULE: If ACTION TAKEN is 'halt' and the EVIDENCE GATHERED contains a concrete success marker ([READ_CHUNK_OK], [SEARCH_CODE_OK], [READ_OK], [WRITE_OK], [DIFF_PATCH_OK], [PATCH_FUNCTION_OK], [CMD_OK]), this is a CORRECT completion -- give a positive modifier (+0.50), NOT negative.\nDoes this evidence represent meaningful progress toward the strategy? If it gathers useful context, give a positive modifier.\nOUTPUT ONLY VALID JSON:\n{{\"critique\": \"<reason>\", \"confidence_modifier\": <float from -0.5 to 0.5>}}", strategy, action, truncated_evidence);
        let payload = serde_json::json!({
            "contents": [{"role": "user", "parts": [{"text": prompt}]}],
            "generationConfig": {
                "temperature": 0.0,
                "responseMimeType": "application/json",
                "maxOutputTokens": 4096,
                "responseSchema": {
                    "type": "OBJECT",
                    "properties": {
                        "critique": { "type": "STRING" },
                        "confidence_modifier": { "type": "NUMBER" }
                    },
                    "required": ["critique", "confidence_modifier"]
                }
            }
        });
        let text = Self::safe_api_call(
            client,
            api_key,
            &crate::model_config::get_light_models(),
            &payload,
        )
        .await?;
        let clean_text = sanitize_json_escapes(&text);
        serde_json::from_str(&clean_text).map_err(|e| anyhow!("Parse error: {}", e))
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/executive_cortex.rs

use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::orchestrator_v2::task_graph::TaskGraph;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub enum ExecutiveGoalStatus {
    Active,
    Suspended,
    Completed,
    Abandoned,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct GoalNode {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub objective: String,
    pub belief_score: f32,
    pub status: ExecutiveGoalStatus,
    pub children: Vec<Uuid>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum ExecutiveCommand {
    InjectGoal { objective: String, parent_id: Option<Uuid> },
    EvaluateEnvironment,
    EmergencyHalt { reason: String },
}

pub struct ExecutiveCortex {
    goals: HashMap<Uuid, GoalNode>,
    active_graph: Option<TaskGraph>,
    memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    command_rx: mpsc::Receiver<ExecutiveCommand>,
}

impl ExecutiveCortex {
    pub fn new(memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>) -> (Self, mpsc::Sender<ExecutiveCommand>) {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(100);
        (Self { goals: HashMap::new(), active_graph: None, memory, command_rx: rx }, tx)
    }

    pub async fn run_actor_loop(mut self) {
        // Run a pulse every 10 seconds for persistent state checking
        let mut heartbeat = tokio::time::interval(std::time::Duration::from_secs(10));

        loop {
            tokio::select! {
                Some(cmd) = self.command_rx.recv() => {
                    self.handle_command(cmd).await;
                }
                _ = heartbeat.tick() => {
                    self.cognitive_tick().await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn cognitive_tick(&mut self) {
        let active_goals: Vec<&GoalNode> =
            self.goals.values().filter(|g| g.status == ExecutiveGoalStatus::Active).collect();

        if active_goals.is_empty() {
            return;
        }

        tracing::info!(
            "[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Pulse check: Maintaining intent vector for {} active goal(s)...",
            active_goals.len()
        );

        for goal in active_goals {
            let graph_json = serde_json::to_string(&self.active_graph).unwrap_or_default();
            let status_str = format!("{:?}", goal.status);
            let created_at = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .as_secs() as i64;

            // Persist the active intention to SQLite, allowing it to survive reboots
            let _ = self
                .memory
                .upsert_plan_run(
                    &goal.id.to_string(),
                    &goal.objective,
                    &graph_json,
                    &status_str,
                    created_at,
                )
                .await;
        }
    }

    pub async fn evaluate_belief_state(
        &self,
        belief_id: uuid::Uuid,
        confidence: f32,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision> {
        let decision = crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecisionPolicy::evaluate(belief_id, confidence);

        self.memory.store_belief_decision(decision.clone()).await?;

        Ok(decision)
    }

    pub async fn apply_belief_decision(
        &mut self,
        goal_id: uuid::Uuid,
        decision: crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationDecision> {
        let adaptation = crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationEngine::from_belief_action(
            goal_id,
            decision.action,
        );

        match adaptation.action {
            crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationAction::PauseGoal => {
                if let Some(goal) = self.goals.get_mut(&goal_id) {
                    goal.status = ExecutiveGoalStatus::Suspended;
                }
            }

            crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationAction::TerminateGoal => {
                if let Some(goal) = self.goals.get_mut(&goal_id) {
                    goal.status = ExecutiveGoalStatus::Abandoned;
                }
            }

            _ => {}
        }

        self.memory.store_goal_adaptation(adaptation.clone()).await?;

        Ok(adaptation)
    }

    async fn handle_command(&mut self, cmd: ExecutiveCommand) {
        match cmd {
            ExecutiveCommand::InjectGoal { objective, parent_id } => {
                let id = Uuid::new_v4();
                tracing::info!(
                    "\x1b[35m[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Injecting new background goal: {}\x1b[0m",
                    objective
                );

                let goal = GoalNode {
                    id,
                    objective,
                    belief_score: 0.5, // Seed with standard prior
                    status: ExecutiveGoalStatus::Active,
                    children: vec![],
                };
                self.goals.insert(id, goal);

                if let Some(pid) = parent_id {
                    if let Some(parent) = self.goals.get_mut(&pid) {
                        parent.children.push(id);
                    }
                }
            }
            ExecutiveCommand::EmergencyHalt { reason } => {
                tracing::error!(
                    "\x1b[31m[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] GLOBAL HALT TRIGGERED: {}\x1b[0m",
                    reason
                );
                self.goals.values_mut().for_each(|g| g.status = ExecutiveGoalStatus::Suspended);
            }
            ExecutiveCommand::EvaluateEnvironment => {
                tracing::info!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Environment evaluation requested.");
            }
        }
    }
}

pub fn evaluate_causal_memory(memories: Vec<crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall>) -> Vec<String> {
    memories.iter().map(|m| m.recommendation()).collect()
}

// v1.9 hard evidence gate:
// Before marking a GoalNode / strategy as Proven, call:
//   belief.may_prove(required_artifacts, threshold)
// Never treat scalar confidence alone as sufficient.

use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use anyhow::Result;

/// Public shared JSON string escape sanitizer.
pub fn sanitize_json_escapes(raw: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(raw.len());
    let mut in_string = false;
    let mut chars = raw.chars().peekable();

    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        match c {
            '"' => {
                in_string = !in_string;
                out.push('"');
            }
            '\n' if in_string => {
                out.push_str("\\n");
            }
            '\r' if in_string => {
                out.push_str("\\r");
            }
            '\\' => {
                if let Some(&next) = chars.peek() {
                    if next == '"'
                        || next == '\\'
                        || next == '/'
                        || next == 'b'
                        || next == 'f'
                        || next == 'n'
                        || next == 'r'
                        || next == 't'
                        || next == 'u'
                    {
                        out.push('\\');
                    } else {
                        out.push_str("\\\\");
                    }
                } else {
                    out.push_str("\\\\");
                }
            }
            _ => out.push(c),
        }
    }
    out
}

pub fn extract_actions(response: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentAction>> {
    let text = response.trim();

    if text.is_empty() || text == "[]" {
        return Ok(vec![]);
    }

    let trimmed = text.replace("```json", "").replace("```", "");
    let clean_text = sanitize_json_escapes(trimmed.trim());

    if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(&clean_text) {
        return Ok(actions);
    }

    if let Ok(single) = serde_json::from_str::<AgentAction>(&clean_text) {
        return Ok(vec![single]);
    }

    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (clean_text.find('['), clean_text.rfind(']')) {
        if start < end {
            let slice = &clean_text[start..=end];
            if let Ok(actions) = serde_json::from_str::<Vec<AgentAction>>(slice) {
                return Ok(actions);
            }
        }
    }

    if let (Some(start), Some(end)) = (clean_text.find('{'), clean_text.rfind('}')) {
        if start < end {
            let slice = &clean_text[start..=end];
            if let Ok(single) = serde_json::from_str::<AgentAction>(slice) {
                return Ok(vec![single]);
            }
        }
    }

    tracing::warn!("[JSON PARSER] Could not parse action payload. Falling back safely.");
    Ok(vec![])
}

use serde::de::DeserializeOwned;

pub fn safe_parse_json<T: DeserializeOwned>(raw: &str) -> Result<T, serde_json::Error> {
    // 1. Strip rogue Markdown formatting often hallucinated by models
    let mut s = raw.trim();
    if s.starts_with("```json") {
        s = s.trim_start_matches("```json").trim_end_matches("```").trim();
    } else if s.starts_with("```") {
        s = s.trim_start_matches("```").trim_end_matches("```").trim();
    }

    // 2. Scan and repair invalid escape sequences (e.g., regex inside JSON strings)
    let mut fixed = String::with_capacity(s.len() + 10);
    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
    
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        if c == '\\' {
            if let Some(&next_c) = chars.peek() {
                match next_c {
                    'n' | 't' | 'r' | '"' | '\\' | '/' | 'u' | 'b' | 'f' => {
                        // Valid JSON escape sequence
                        fixed.push('\\');
                    },
                    _ => {
                        // Invalid escape sequence detected. Double-escape it to protect the parser.
                        fixed.push('\\');
                        fixed.push('\\'); 
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Trailing backslash
                fixed.push('\\');
                fixed.push('\\');
            }
        } else {
            fixed.push(c);
        }
    }
    
    // 3. Attempt to parse the repaired string, fallback to original if structural failure
    serde_json::from_str(&fixed).or_else(|_| serde_json::from_str(s))
}

use crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory;
use std::sync::Arc;

use anyhow::Result;
use tokio::sync::RwLock;

use nexus_substrate::memory::{AgentMemory, MemoryPalace};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct MemoryCortex {
    palace: Arc<MemoryPalace>,
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub fn new(palace: Arc<MemoryPalace>) -> Self {
        Self { palace }
    }

    pub async fn store_memory(
        &self,
        category: &str,
        key: &str,
        value: &str,
        embedding: Option<Vec<f32>>,
    ) -> Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace;

        mem.store_memory(category, key, value, embedding).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn search_memories(&self, query: &str) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>> {
        let mem = self.palace;

        let result = mem.search_memories(query).await?;

        Ok(result)
    }

    pub async fn semantic_search(&self, embedding: &[f32]) -> Result<Vec<AgentMemory>> {
        let mem = self.palace;

        let result = mem.semantic_search(embedding).await?;

        Ok(result)
    }

    pub async fn remember(&self, memory: CognitiveMemory) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace;

        mem.store_memory("cognitive", &memory.key, &memory.content, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn upsert_plan_run(
        &self,
        id: &str,
        objective: &str,
        graph: &str,
        status: &str,
        created_at: i64,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let mem = self.palace;

        mem.upsert_plan_run(id, objective, graph, status, created_at).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn consolidate_memory(
        &self,
        memory: crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let score = crate::memory_consolidation::MemoryConsolidator::calculate_retention(&memory);

        if score < 0.1 {
            return Ok(false);
        }

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(true)
    }

    pub async fn reflect_and_store(
        &self,
        memories: Vec<crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory>,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let engine = crate::reflection_engine::ReflectionEngine::new();

        if let Some(reflection) = engine.reflect(&memories) {
            self.remember(reflection).await?;

            return Ok(true);
        }

        Ok(false)
    }

    pub async fn recall(
        &self,
        context: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::memory_recall::RecallResult> {
        let memories = self
            .search_memories(context)
            .await?
            .into_iter()
            .take(limit)
            .map(|m| crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
                id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),
                kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Experience,
                key: m.directive.clone(),
                content: m.result.clone(),
                source: m.origin.clone(),
                confidence: 0.5,
                importance: 0.5,
                created_at: m
                    .timestamp
                    .parse::<i64>()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp()),
                tags: vec![m.origin.clone()],
            })
            .collect();

        let engine = crate::memory_recall::MemoryRecallEngine::new();

        Ok(engine.rank(memories, context))
    }

    pub async fn store_belief_update(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_feedback::BeliefUpdateEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&event)?;

        self.store_memory("belief_update", &event.belief_id.to_string(), &payload, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_belief_decision(
        &self,
        decision: crate::belief_policy::BeliefDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&decision)?;

        self.store_memory("belief_decision", &decision.belief_id.to_string(), &payload, None)
            .await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn store_goal_adaptation(
        &self,
        decision: crate::goal_adaptation::GoalAdaptationDecision,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&decision)?;

        self.store_memory("goal_adaptation", &decision.goal_id.to_string(), &payload, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn health_check(&self) -> anyhow::Result<bool> {
        let _mem = self.palace;

        Ok(true)
    }

    pub async fn emit_belief_update(
        &self,
        belief_id: &str,
        evidence: &str,
        confidence_delta: f32,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<crate::belief_update::BeliefUpdate> {
        Ok(crate::belief_update::BeliefUpdate::new(belief_id, evidence, confidence_delta))
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_semantic_memory(
        &self,
        memory: crate::memory_semantic::SemanticMemory,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let content = serde_json::to_string(&memory)?;

        self.store_memory("semantic", &memory.lesson, &content, None).await?;

        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn recall_semantic(
        &self,
        category: &str,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<nexus_substrate::memory::AgentMemory>> {
        self.search_memories(category).await
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_belief_transition(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_transition::BeliefTransitionEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_transition_{}", event.belief_id),

            content: event.lesson,

            source: "belief_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: event.new_confidence,

            importance: 0.9,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec!["belief".to_string(), "reflection".to_string(), "adaptation".to_string()],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_belief_cause(
        &self,
        cause: crate::belief_causality::BeliefCause,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_cause_{}", cause.id),

            content: format!("BELIEF CAUSE: {} | LESSON: {}", cause.cause, cause.lesson),

            source: "belief_causality_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: cause.new_confidence,

            importance: 0.95,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec![
                "belief".to_string(),
                "causal_memory".to_string(),
                "reflection".to_string(),
                "adaptation".to_string(),
            ],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn recall_causal_pattern(
        &self,
        observation: String,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<Vec<crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall>> {
        let mut results = Vec::new();

        if observation.to_lowercase().contains("memory") {
            results.push(crate::causal_recall::CausalRecall {
                pattern: "memory".to_string(),

                previous_cause: "resource exhaustion".to_string(),

                lesson: "Use resource-aware routing before execution".to_string(),

                confidence: 0.95,
            });
        }

        Ok(results)
    }
}

impl MemoryCortex {
    pub async fn store_validation_event(
        &self,
        event: crate::belief_validation::BeliefValidationEvent,
    ) -> anyhow::Result<()> {
        let memory = crate::memory_types::CognitiveMemory {
            id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),

            kind: crate::memory_types::MemoryKind::Reflection,

            key: format!("belief_validation_{}", uuid::Uuid::new_v4()),

            content: event.lesson,

            source: "belief_validation_engine".to_string(),

            confidence: 1.0 - event.error_score,

            importance: 0.95,

            created_at: chrono::Utc::now().timestamp(),

            tags: vec![
                "belief".into(),
                "prediction".into(),
                "validation".into(),
                "causal_learning".into(),
            ],
        };

        self.remember(memory).await?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

// --- PHASE 2: STATEFUL WORKING MEMORY & CACHE ---
use std::collections::hash_map::DefaultHasher;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::hash::{Hash, Hasher};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct WorkingMemory {
    pub active_node: uuid::Uuid,
    pub required_objectives: Vec<String>,
    pub completed_objectives: Vec<String>,
}

impl WorkingMemory {
    pub fn new(active_node: uuid::Uuid, objectives: Vec<String>) -> Self {
        Self { active_node, required_objectives: objectives, completed_objectives: Vec::new() }
    }

    pub fn mark_completed(&mut self, objective: &str) {
        if let Some(pos) = self.required_objectives.iter().position(|x| x == objective) {
            let obj = self.required_objectives.remove(pos);
            self.completed_objectives.push(obj);
        }
    }

    pub fn get_prompt_context(&self) -> String {
        format!(
            "[WORKING MEMORY]\nPending Objectives: {:?}\nCompleted Objectives: {:?}",
            self.required_objectives, self.completed_objectives
        )
    }

    pub fn is_mission_accomplished(&self) -> bool {
        self.required_objectives.is_empty()
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct CachedAction {
    pub output: String,
    pub exit_status: i32,
    pub timestamp: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct ActionCache {
    cache: HashMap<String, CachedAction>,
}

impl ActionCache {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { cache: HashMap::new() }
    }

    pub fn generate_key(command: &str, ast_hash: &str) -> String {
        let mut s = DefaultHasher::new();
        command.hash(&mut s);
        ast_hash.hash(&mut s);
        s.finish().to_string()
    }

    pub fn get(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&CachedAction> {
        self.cache.get(key)
    }

    pub fn insert(&mut self, key: String, output: String, exit_status: i32) {
        let timestamp = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs();
        self.cache.insert(key, CachedAction { output, exit_status, timestamp });
    }
}

use std::env;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};

static GEMINI_DISABLED_UNTIL: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);
static NVIDIA_DISABLED_UNTIL: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

pub fn report_rate_limit(model_name: &str) {
    let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs();
    let cooldown = 60; // Cooldown 60s

    if model_name.contains("gemini") || model_name.contains("gemma") {
        GEMINI_DISABLED_UNTIL.store(now + cooldown, Ordering::Relaxed);
        tracing::warn!("[CIRCUIT BREAKER] Gemini/Gemma rate-limited (429). Bypassing for 60s.");
    } else if model_name.contains("nvidia") || model_name.contains("nemotron") {
        NVIDIA_DISABLED_UNTIL.store(now + cooldown, Ordering::Relaxed);
        tracing::warn!("[CIRCUIT BREAKER] NVIDIA rate-limited (429). Bypassing for 60s.");
    }
}

pub fn get_light_models() -> Vec<String> {
    let mut healthy = Vec::new();
    let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
        .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
        .unwrap_or_default()
        .as_secs();

    // 1. .env overrides
    if let Ok(m) = env::var("NEXUS_LIGHT_MODEL") {
        if !m.trim().is_empty() && !m.contains("mistral-nemo") {
            healthy.push(m.trim().to_string());
        }
    }
    if let Ok(m) = env::var("NEXUS_PLANNER_MODEL") {
        if !m.trim().is_empty()
            && !m.contains("mistral-nemo")
            && !healthy.contains(&m.trim().to_string())
        {
            healthy.push(m.trim().to_string());
        }
    }

    // 2. NVIDIA Nemotron (Verified 200 OK)
    let nv_key = env::var("NVIDIA_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default();
    if !nv_key.trim().is_empty()
        && now > NVIDIA_DISABLED_UNTIL.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
        && !healthy.contains(&"nvidia/nemotron-3-ultra-550b-a55b".to_string())
    {
        healthy.push("nvidia/nemotron-3-ultra-550b-a55b".to_string());
    }

    // 3. Google Gemma (Verified 200 OK)
    let gemini_key = env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default();
    if !gemini_key.trim().is_empty()
        && now > GEMINI_DISABLED_UNTIL.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
        && !healthy.contains(&"gemma-4-31b-it".to_string())
    {
        healthy.push("gemma-4-31b-it".to_string());
    }

    if healthy.is_empty() {
        healthy.push("nvidia/nemotron-3-ultra-550b-a55b".to_string());
        healthy.push("gemma-4-31b-it".to_string());
    }

    healthy
}

pub fn get_heavy_models() -> Vec<String> {

    let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()

    healthy

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/podman.rs

use std::io::{Error, ErrorKind, Result};
use std::process::{Command, Output};

pub struct PodmanShield;
impl PodmanShield {
    pub fn execute(command: &str) -> Result<Output> {
        if let Err(e) =
            crate::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent::validate_action("execute_command", command)
        {
            return Err(Error::new(ErrorKind::PermissionDenied, e));
        }
        // Use bash, not POSIX sh -- the Strategist routinely proposes bash-only
        // syntax (${PIPESTATUS[0]}, the `time` keyword, etc.) which fails under
        // sh with cryptic errors like "Bad substitution", wasting turns before
        // any working command is found.
        let mut child = Command::new("bash");
        child.arg("-c").arg(command);
        for (key, value) in std::env::vars() {
            if key != "CARGO_BUILD_TARGET" {
                child.env(key, value);
            }
        }
        child.env_remove("CARGO_BUILD_TARGET");
        let out = child.output()?;

        // REGRESSION FIX (reapplied): explicitly check command success and
        // expose stderr. Without this, a failing command (e.g. a broken
        // `cargo check`) is indistinguishable from a successful one -- both
        // get tagged [CMD_OK] by the executor, which the Cortical Loop's
        // is_terminal_cmd heuristic then treats as proof of success.
        if !out.status.success() {
            let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr);
            return Err(Error::other(format!(
                "Command failed with status: {}\nSTDERR:\n{}",
                out.status, stderr
            )));
        }

        Ok(out)
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum Capability {
    ReadSubstrate,
    WriteSubstrate,
    ExecuteSystemCall,
    HaltCognition,
}

pub struct ExecutionSandbox {
    pub active_capabilities: Vec<Capability>,
}

impl ExecutionSandbox {
    pub fn strict_lockdown() -> Self {
        Self { active_capabilities: vec![Capability::ReadSubstrate] }
    }

    pub fn validate_intent(&self, action_type: &str) -> Result<(), String> {
        let required_token = match action_type {
            "read_file" | "read_file_chunk" | "search_code" | "search_memory" | "read_function" => {
                Capability::ReadSubstrate
            }
            "write_file" | "diff_patch" | "patch_function" | "lazarus_snapshot"
            | "export_report" => Capability::WriteSubstrate,
            "execute_command" | "git_rollback" => Capability::ExecuteSystemCall,
            "halt" => Capability::HaltCognition,
            _ => return Err(format!("SECURITY_FAULT: Unknown action taxonomy '{}'", action_type)),
        };

        if self.active_capabilities.contains(&required_token) {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(format!("POLICY_VIOLATION: Agent lacks {:?} capability.", required_token))
        }
    }
}

/// Explicit Machine-Readable Tool Contracts to prevent CLI flag-guessing and false-positive Critic vetoes.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize)]
pub struct ToolContract {
    pub name: &'static str,
    pub binary_path: &'static str,
    pub flags: &'static [&'static str],
    pub usage_pattern: &'static str,
}

        name: "execute_command",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["command"],

    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "search_memory",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["query"],

    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "search_code",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["path", "query"],
        usage_pattern: "search_code <path> <query> (Fast recursive search for keywords/patterns across all files in a directory)",
    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "read_file",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["path"],
        usage_pattern: "read_file <filepath>",
    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "read_file_chunk",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["path", "start_line", "max_lines"],
        usage_pattern: "read_file_chunk <filepath> <start_line> <max_lines> (paginated read for files too large for read_file)",
    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "write_file",
        binary_path: "internal::agent_action",
        flags: &["path", "content"],
        usage_pattern: "write_file <filepath> <content>",
    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "benchmark_runner",
        binary_path: "target/release/benchmark_runner",
        flags: &["--target", "--output", "--iterations"],
        usage_pattern: "benchmark_runner --target <symbol> --output /tmp/benchmark.json OR benchmark_runner <target> --output /tmp/benchmark.json",
    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "impact_analyzer",

    },
    ToolContract {
        name: "mutation_ranker",
        binary_path: "target/release/mutation_ranker",
        flags: &["--candidate", "--impact-report", "--health-report", "--output"],
        usage_pattern: "mutation_ranker --candidate <target> --impact-report /tmp/impact.json --health-report /tmp/audit.json --output /tmp/verdict.json",
    },
];

use anyhow::Result;
use std::path::Path;
use wasmtime::{Caller, Engine, Linker, Module, Store};
use wasmtime_wasi::p1::{self, WasiP1Ctx};
use wasmtime_wasi::WasiCtxBuilder;

// 1. Define a custom state that holds BOTH WASI and your own host resources
pub struct HostState {
    pub wasi: WasiP1Ctx,
    // Add your turbovec index or other host resources here later:
    // pub vector_vault: Option<std::sync::Arc<VectorVault>>,
}

pub struct SidecarHost {
    engine: Engine,
}

impl SidecarHost {
    pub fn new(_dummy: bool) -> Result<Self> {
        let mut config = wasmtime::Config::new();
        config.wasm_backtrace_details(wasmtime::WasmBacktraceDetails::Enable);
        let engine = Engine::new(&config)?;
        Ok(Self { engine })
    }

    pub fn load_and_run(&self, wasm_path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
        let module = Module::from_file(&self.engine, wasm_path)?;

        // 2. Linker must now be typed to our custom HostState
        let mut linker: Linker<HostState> = Linker::new(&self.engine);

        // 3. Link WASI by providing a closure to extract WasiP1Ctx from HostState
        p1::add_to_linker_sync(&mut linker, |state: &mut HostState| &mut state.wasi)?;

        // =====================================================================
        // ⚡ HOST FUNCTION BRIDGE: This is where we expose Rust to Wasm
        // =====================================================================
        linker.func_wrap(
            "env",
            "search_memories",
            |_caller: Caller<'_, HostState>, ptr: u32, dim: u32| -> u32 {
                println!(
                    ">> [HOST] Intercepted Wasm call to search_memories(ptr: {}, dim: {})",
                    ptr, dim
                );

                // Memory bridge logic goes here...
                // 1. Read float array from caller.get_export("memory")
                // 2. Pass to turbovec
                // 3. Write results back

                0 // Return memory pointer of results
            },
        )?;
        // =====================================================================

        let wasi = WasiCtxBuilder::new().inherit_stdio().build_p1();

        // 4. Initialize the Store with our composite HostState
        let mut store = Store::new(&self.engine, HostState { wasi });

        let instance = linker.instantiate(&mut store, &module)?;

        let start = instance.get_typed_func::<(), ()>(&mut store, "_start")?;
        start.call(&mut store, ())?;

        Ok(())
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/tools/file_writer.rs

use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;
use anyhow::{Result, Context};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize, Debug)]
pub struct WriteFileInput {
    pub path: String,
    pub content: String,
}

pub fn execute_write_file(input: WriteFileInput) -> Result<String> {
    let path = Path::new(&input.path);
    
    if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
        if !parent.as_os_str().is_empty() {
            fs::create_dir_all(parent)
                .with_context(|| format!("Failed to create parent directories for {}", input.path))?;
        }
    }

    fs::write(path, &input.content)
        .with_context(|| format!("Failed to write file contents to {}", input.path))?;

    tracing::info!("[EVENT] Mission Complete. AST mutation seamlessly integrated.");

    Ok(format!("Successfully wrote {} bytes to target path: {}", input.content.len(), input.path))
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/verification.rs 
=========================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct VerificationReport {
    pub evidence_quality: f32,
    pub mutation_detected: bool,
    pub command_completed: bool,
    pub security_violation_detected: bool,
    pub goal_verified: bool,
}

impl VerificationReport {
    pub fn from_evidence(evidence: &str, evaluator_confidence: f32) -> Self {
        let security_violation = evidence.contains("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED")
            || evidence.contains("sandbox_violation")
            || evidence.contains("SECURITY_VIOLATION");

        let command_completed = (evidence.contains("[CMD_OK]")
            || evidence.contains("[READ_OK]")
            || evidence.contains("[WRITE_OK]"))
            && !security_violation;

        Self {
            evidence_quality: if security_violation { 0.0 } else { evaluator_confidence.max(0.0) },
            mutation_detected: evidence.contains("[WRITE_OK]")
                || evidence.contains("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]")
                || evidence.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]"),
            command_completed,
            security_violation_detected: security_violation,
            goal_verified: evaluator_confidence > 0.5 && !security_violation,
        }
    }

    pub fn can_complete(&self, belief: f32) -> bool {
        !self.security_violation_detected
            && self.command_completed
            && belief >= 0.85
            && self.goal_verified
            && self.evidence_quality >= 0.5
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::process::Command;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct WorkspaceNode {
    pub path: String,
    pub kind: String,
    pub deps: Vec<String>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct WorldModel {
    pub nodes: HashMap<String, WorkspaceNode>,
    pub last_refresh: u64,
}

impl WorldModel {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub async fn refresh(&mut self) -> Result<()> {
        self.last_refresh =
            std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn get_summary(&self) -> String {
        format!("Workspace: {} nodes. Main binary: ide_backend.", self.nodes.len())
    }

    pub async fn get_topology() -> Option<String> {
        None
    }

    pub async fn semantic_query(&self, _mission: &str) -> Vec<String> {
        let mut chunks = Vec::new();

        // 1. Inject Repository File Tree Context
        if let Ok(out) = Command::new("find")
            .args([
                ".",
                "-maxdepth",
                "4",
                "-type",
                "f",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/.git/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/target/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/node_modules/*",
                "-not",
                "-path",
                "*/.cold_storage/*",
            ])
            .output()
        {
            if out.status.success() {
                let files = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout);
                chunks.push("[REPOSITORY FILE TREE (Depth 4)]".to_string());
                chunks.push(files.chars().take(6000).collect());
            }
        }

        // 2. Inject Verified Machine-Readable Tool Contracts into Context
        chunks.push("\n[AVAILABLE WORKSPACE TOOL CONTRACTS]".to_string());
        for tool in crate::policy::TOOL_CONTRACTS {
            chunks.push(format!(
                "• Tool '{}': Usage -> {}\n  Binary -> {}",
                tool.name, tool.usage_pattern, tool.binary_path
            ));
        }

        chunks
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/completion_engine.rs 
=========================================
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, PartialEq)]
pub enum CompletionCondition {
    ReadMarkerAcquired,
    SearchMarkerAcquired,
    WriteMarkerAcquired,
    PatchMarkerAcquired,
    CommandMarkerAcquired,
    MutationVerified,
    MinBeliefScore(f32),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SubgoalContract {
    pub node_id: String,
    pub objective: String,
    pub conditions: Vec<CompletionCondition>,
    pub is_mutation_task: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveBudget {
    pub max_turns: u32,
    pub max_model_calls: u32,
    pub model_calls_made: u32,
}

impl CognitiveBudget {
    pub fn default_for_node() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_turns: 8,
            max_model_calls: 15,
            model_calls_made: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn record_call(&mut self) {
        self.model_calls_made += 1;
    }

    pub fn is_exhausted(&self, current_turn: u32) -> bool {
        current_turn >= self.max_turns || self.model_calls_made >= self.max_model_calls
    }
}

pub struct SubgoalCompletionEngine;

impl SubgoalCompletionEngine {
    pub fn infer_contract(node_id: &str, objective: &str) -> SubgoalContract {
        let obj_lower = objective.to_lowercase();
        let mut conditions = Vec::new();
        let mut is_mutation_task = false;

        if obj_lower.contains("write") || obj_lower.contains("export") || obj_lower.contains("save") || obj_lower.contains("output") {
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::WriteMarkerAcquired);
            is_mutation_task = true;
        } else if obj_lower.contains("patch") || obj_lower.contains("apply") || obj_lower.contains("fix") || obj_lower.contains("remediate") {
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::PatchMarkerAcquired);
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::MutationVerified);
            is_mutation_task = true;
        } else if obj_lower.contains("search") || obj_lower.contains("find") || obj_lower.contains("locate") {
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::SearchMarkerAcquired);
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::MinBeliefScore(0.65));
        } else if obj_lower.contains("read") || obj_lower.contains("inspect") || obj_lower.contains("audit") {
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::ReadMarkerAcquired);
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::MinBeliefScore(0.65));
        } else {
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::CommandMarkerAcquired);
            conditions.push(CompletionCondition::MinBeliefScore(0.65));
        }

        SubgoalContract {
            node_id: node_id.to_string(),
            objective: objective.to_string(),
            conditions,
            is_mutation_task,
        }
    }

    pub fn evaluate(contract: &SubgoalContract, local_evidence: &str, belief_score: f32) -> bool {
        for cond in &contract.conditions {
            match cond {
                CompletionCondition::WriteMarkerAcquired => {
                    if !local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]")) {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::PatchMarkerAcquired => {
                    let has_patch = local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]"));
                    if !has_patch {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::SearchMarkerAcquired => {
                    let has_search = local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[SEARCH_CODE_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[SEARCH_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_CHUNK_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_OK]"));
                    if !has_search {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::ReadMarkerAcquired => {
                    let has_read = local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_CHUNK_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[SEARCH_CODE_OK]"));
                    if !has_read {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::CommandMarkerAcquired => {
                    let has_cmd = local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[CMD_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_CHUNK_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[SEARCH_CODE_OK]"))
                        || local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_OK]"));
                    if !has_cmd {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::MutationVerified => {
                    let ev_lower = local_evidence.to_lowercase();
                    let verified = ev_lower.contains("finished `dev` profile") 
                        || ev_lower.contains("finished release")
                        || ev_lower.contains("test result: ok")
                        || local_evidence.contains("[MUTATION_CONTRACT_VERIFIED]");
                    if !verified {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
                CompletionCondition::MinBeliefScore(min) => {
                    if belief_score < *min {
                        return false;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        true
    }
}

FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/action_gate.rs
