use crate::motor_cortex::{MotorAction, MotorCommand};
use crate::policy::ExecutionSandbox;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};

pub struct ActionGate {
    sandbox: ExecutionSandbox,
}

impl ActionGate {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            sandbox: ExecutionSandbox {
            active_capabilities: vec![
                crate::policy::Capability::ReadSubstrate,
                crate::policy::Capability::WriteSubstrate,
                crate::policy::Capability::ExecuteSystemCall,
                crate::policy::Capability::HaltCognition,
            ],
        },
        }
    }

    /// Enforces deterministic security inspection on sealed MotorCommands.
    pub fn approve(&self, command: &MotorCommand) -> Result<()> {
        match &command.action {
            MotorAction::ExecuteCommand { command: cmd_str } => {
                let cmd_lower = cmd_str.to_lowercase();
                if cmd_lower.contains("rm -rf /") || cmd_lower.contains("rm -rf ~") || cmd_lower.contains("mkfs") {
                    return Err(anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_SECURITY_VIOLATION: Destructive command blocked: '{}'", cmd_str));
                }
                if cmd_lower.contains("cat .env") || cmd_lower.contains("env |") || cmd_lower.contains("printenv") {
                    return Err(anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_SECURITY_VIOLATION: Secret inspection command blocked: '{}'", cmd_str));
                }
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("execute_command")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::WriteFile { path, content } => {
                if path.contains("..") {
                    return Err(anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_SECURITY_VIOLATION: Path traversal detected: '{}'", path));
                }
                if content.contains("GEMINI_API_KEY=") || content.contains("NVIDIA_API_KEY=") {
                    return Err(anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_SECURITY_VIOLATION: Plaintext API key leak blocked in WriteFile."));
                }
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("write_file")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::ReadFile { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("read_file")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::ReadFileChunk { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("read_file_chunk")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::SearchCode { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("search_code")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::DiffPatch { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("diff_patch")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::PatchFunction { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("patch_function")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::SearchMemory { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("search_memory")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }

            MotorAction::Halt { .. } => {
                self.sandbox.validate_intent("halt")
                    .map_err(|e| anyhow!("ACTION_GATE_POLICY_FAULT: {}", e))?;
            }
        }

        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Default for ActionGate {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/executor.rs 
=========================================
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::motor_cortex::{MotorAction, MotorCommand};
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq, Clone)]
pub enum FsmState {
    Idle,
    Executing,
    Complete,
    Fault,
}

pub struct RoboticExecutor {
    pub current_state: FsmState,
    pub api_key: String,
    #[allow(dead_code)]
    tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub action_cache: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<crate::memory_cortex::ActionCache>>,
}

fn canonicalize_or_parent(p: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    if let Ok(c) = std::fs::canonicalize(p) {
        return Ok(c);
    }
    let parent = p.parent().ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("no parent directory for {}", p.display()))?;
    let parent_canon = std::fs::canonicalize(parent)?;
    let file_name = p.file_name().ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("no file name for {}", p.display()))?;
    Ok(parent_canon.join(file_name))
}

pub fn sanitize(path: &str) -> Result<PathBuf> {
    if path.starts_with("/tmp/") {
        return Ok(Path::new(path).to_path_buf());
    }

    let workspace_root = std::env::current_dir()
        .and_then(std::fs::canonicalize)
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED: could not resolve workspace root ({})", e))?;

    let mut normalized_path = path.to_string();

    if let Some(ws_str) = workspace_root.to_str() {
        if normalized_path.starts_with(ws_str) {
            normalized_path =
                normalized_path.trim_start_matches(ws_str).trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
        }
    }

    if normalized_path.starts_with("src/") {
        normalized_path = format!("crates/runtime_core/{}", normalized_path);
    } else if normalized_path.starts_with("runtime_core/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("nexus_substrate/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("nexus_model_hub/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("nexus_search/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("memory_graph/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("protocol/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("executive_cortex/")
    {
        normalized_path = format!("crates/{}", normalized_path);
    } else if normalized_path.starts_with("ide_backend/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("cli/")
        || normalized_path.starts_with("node/")
    {
        normalized_path = format!("bins/{}", normalized_path);
    }

    let p = Path::new(&normalized_path);
    let target = if p.is_absolute() { p.to_path_buf() } else { workspace_root.join(p) };

    if !target.exists() {
        let crate_fallback = workspace_root.join("crates/runtime_core/src").join(p);
        if crate_fallback.exists() {
            return Ok(crate_fallback);
        }

        if let Some(filename) = p.file_name().and_then(|f| f.to_str()) {
            let lower = filename.to_lowercase();
            if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&workspace_root) {
                for entry in entries.flatten() {
                    if entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_lowercase() == lower {
                        return Ok(entry.path());
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        let crates_fallback = workspace_root.join("crates");
        if crates_fallback.exists() {
            return Ok(crates_fallback);
        }
    }

    let canonical =
        canonicalize_or_parent(&target).map_err(|_| anyhow!("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED: {}", path))?;

    if !canonical.starts_with(&workspace_root) {
        return Err(anyhow!("PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED: {} resolves outside the workspace root", path));
    }

    Ok(canonical)
}

impl RoboticExecutor {
    pub fn new(api_key: String, tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            current_state: FsmState::Idle,
            api_key,
            tx,
            action_cache: Arc::new(tokio::sync::Mutex::new(
                crate::memory_cortex::ActionCache::new(),
            )),
        }
    }

    pub async fn execute_motor_command(
        &mut self,
        command: MotorCommand,
        memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        self.execute_single_action(command.action, memory, vmo_state, dist).await
    }

    async fn execute_single_action(
        &mut self,
        action: MotorAction,
        _memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        _vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        _dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let result: Result<String> = async {
            match action {
                MotorAction::ExecuteCommand { command } => {
                    let cache_key = crate::memory_cortex::ActionCache::generate_key(&command, "v7_workspace_ast");
                    {
                        let cache = self.action_cache.lock().await;
                        if let Some(cached) = cache.get(&cache_key) {
                            return Ok(format!("[CACHE_HIT] (Cached execution)\n{}", cached.output));
                        }
                    }

                    let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&command)?;
                    let stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned();
                    let stderr = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr).into_owned();

                    let mut stdout_trunc = stdout;
                    let mut stderr_trunc = stderr;
                    if stdout_trunc.len() > 8000 {
                        stdout_trunc.truncate(8000);
                        stdout_trunc.push_str("\n...[STDOUT TRUNCATED: OUTPUT TOO LARGE]");
                    }
                    if stderr_trunc.len() > 8000 {
                        stderr_trunc.truncate(8000);
                        stderr_trunc.push_str("\n...[STDERR TRUNCATED: OUTPUT TOO LARGE]");
                    }

                    let mut res = format!("[CMD_OK] Status: {}\n", out.status);
                    if !stdout_trunc.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str(&format!("STDOUT:\n{}\n", stdout_trunc)); }
                    if !stderr_trunc.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str(&format!("STDERR:\n{}\n", stderr_trunc)); }
                    if stdout_trunc.trim().is_empty() && stderr_trunc.trim().is_empty() { res.push_str("(Command produced no output)\n"); }

                    {
                        let mut cache = self.action_cache.lock().await;
                        cache.insert(cache_key, res.clone(), out.status.code().unwrap_or(0));
                    }

                    Ok(res)
                }
                MotorAction::WriteFile { path, content } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                    if let Ok(existing) = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await {
                        if existing == content {
                            return Ok(format!("[WRITE_NOOP] {} -- content identical to existing file", path));
                        }
                    }
                    if let Some(parent) = safe_path.parent() {
                        let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
                    }
                    tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, content).await?;
                    Ok(format!("[WRITE_OK] {}", path))
                }
                MotorAction::ReadFile { path } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                    let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;
                    let snippet = if content.len() > 8000 { &content[..8000] } else { &content };
                    let mem = _memory;
                    if let Ok(emb) = nexus_substrate::vector_bridge::get_embedding(&self.api_key, snippet).await {
                        let _ = mem.store_memory("ReadFile", &path, &content, Some(emb)).await;
                    } else {
                        let _ = mem.store_memory("ReadFile", &path, &content, None).await;
                    }

                    let return_content = if content.len() > 12000 {
                        format!("{}...\n\n[CONTENT TRUNCATED. FULL DATA SAVED TO VECTOR VAULT.]", &content[..12000])
                    } else {
                        content.clone()
                    };

                    Ok(format!("[READ_OK] {}\nCONTENT PREVIEW:\n{}", path, return_content))
                }
                MotorAction::ReadFileChunk { path, start_line, max_lines } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                    let content = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;
                    let lines: Vec<&str> = content.lines().collect();
                    let total_lines = lines.len();

                    if start_line >= total_lines {
                        return Ok(format!("[READ_CHUNK_EOF] Reached end of file {}. Total lines: {}", path, total_lines));
                    }

                    let end_line = std::cmp::min(start_line + max_lines, total_lines);
                    let chunk = lines[start_line..end_line].join("\n");

                    Ok(format!(
                        "[READ_CHUNK_OK] File: {} | Lines {}-{} of {}\nCONTENT:\n{}",
                        path, start_line, end_line, total_lines, chunk
                    ))
                }
                MotorAction::SearchCode { path, query } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path).unwrap_or_else(|_| PathBuf::from("crates"));
                    let p_str = safe_path.to_str().unwrap_or(&path);

                    let cmd = format!(
                        "(rg -n -C 2 -i '{}' {} || grep -rn -C 2 --exclude-dir={{.git,target,.surgical_backup,.nexus,.lazarus,node_modules}} -iE '{}' {}) 2>/dev/null || true",
                        query, p_str, query, p_str
                    );
                    let out = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&cmd)?;
                    let mut stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).to_string();

                    // ⚡ AUTOMATIC WORKSPACE FALLBACK: If subpath returns 0 matches and query was for cortex/router, search crates/runtime_core/src/
                    if stdout.trim().is_empty() && (p_str.contains("executive_cortex") || query.contains("router")) {
                        let fallback_cmd = format!(
                            "(rg -n -C 2 -i '{}' crates/runtime_core/src/ || grep -rn -C 2 --exclude-dir={{.git,target,.surgical_backup,.nexus,.lazarus,node_modules}} -iE '{}' crates/runtime_core/src/) 2>/dev/null || true",
                            query, query
                        );
                        if let Ok(fb_out) = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&fallback_cmd) {
                            let fb_stdout = String::from_utf8_lossy(&fb_out.stdout);
                            if !fb_stdout.trim().is_empty() {
                                stdout = format!("[FALLBACK MATCHES FROM crates/runtime_core/src/]:\n{}", fb_stdout);
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    let trunc = if stdout.len() > 8000 {
                        format!("{}...\n[MATCHES TRUNCATED: OUTPUT TOO LARGE]", &stdout[..8000])
                    } else {
                        stdout
                    };

                    if trunc.trim().is_empty() {
                        Ok(format!("[SEARCH_CODE_OK] Path: {} | Query: '{}'\nNo code matches found in specified path.", path, query))
                    } else {
                        Ok(format!("[SEARCH_CODE_OK] Path: {} | Query: '{}'\nMATCHES:\n{}", path, query, trunc))
                    }
                }
                MotorAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                    let original = tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe_path).await?;
                    let patched = crate::diff::DiffEngine::apply(&original, &search, &replace)?;
                    tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, patched).await?;
                    Ok(format!("[DIFF_PATCH_OK] Applied changes to {}", path))
                }
                MotorAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body } => {
                    let safe_path = sanitize(&path)?;
                    let patched = crate::ast_editor::AstEditor::update_function(safe_path.to_str().unwrap_or(&path), &function_name, &new_body)?;
                    tokio::fs::write(&safe_path, patched).await?;
                    Ok(format!("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK] Updated function '{}' in {}", function_name, path))
                }
                MotorAction::SearchMemory { query } => {
                    let mem = _memory;
                    let mut results_text = String::new();

                    if let Ok(emb) = nexus_substrate::vector_bridge::get_embedding(&self.api_key, &query).await {
                        if let Ok(results) = mem.semantic_search(&emb).await {
                            for r in results {
                                let snippet = if r.result.len() > 3000 { &r.result[..3000] } else { &r.result };
                                results_text.push_str(&format!("Source: {}\nData: {}...\n---\n", r.directive, snippet));
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    if results_text.is_empty() {
                        Ok(format!("[SEARCH_OK] Native FastEmbed vector recall queried for: '{}'. VectorVault active.", query))
                    } else {
                        Ok(format!("[SEARCH_OK] Found semantic context:\n{}", results_text))
                    }
                }
                MotorAction::Halt { reasoning } => Ok(format!("[HALT] {}", reasoning)),
            }
        }.await;

        result
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/task_manager.rs 
=========================================
use crate::executive_cortex::{ExecutiveCommand, ExecutiveCortex};
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::{
    cortical_loop::{CortexOutcome, CorticalLoop},
    distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler,
    metrics::METRICS,
    orchestrator::Orchestrator,
    orchestrator_v2::planner::PlannerAgent,
    shared_state::VmoState,
    world_model::WorldModel,
};
use reqwest::Client;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};

pub struct TaskContext {
    pub client: Client,
    pub api_key: String,
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
}

pub async fn send_task(
    _orchestrator: Arc<Orchestrator>,
    ctx: TaskContext,
    raw_directive: &str,
) -> String {
    METRICS.inc_task();
    
    // ⚡ 1. EXECUTIVE CORTEX GOAL INJECTION
    let (executive_cortex, command_tx) = ExecutiveCortex::new(ctx.memory.clone());
    let goal_id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4();
    let _ = command_tx.send(ExecutiveCommand::InjectGoal {
        objective: raw_directive.to_string(),
        parent_id: None,
    }).await;

    tokio::spawn(executive_cortex.run_actor_loop());

    let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Registered goal {}. Decomposing DAG...", goal_id)}).to_string());

    let mut graph = match PlannerAgent::generate_plan(&ctx.client, &ctx.api_key, raw_directive).await {
        Ok(g) => g,
        Err(e) => return format!("FRACTURE: Planner failed to decompose objective: {}", e),
    };

    graph.compute_critical_path_priorities();
    let order = match graph.resolve_execution_order() {
        Ok(o) => o,
        Err(e) => return format!("FRACTURE: Graph validation failed: {}", e),
    };

    let plan_id = uuid::Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
    let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[EXECUTIVE CORTEX] Plan {} persisted.", plan_id)}).to_string());

    let mut task_outputs: HashMap<String, String> = HashMap::new();
    let mut mission_had_failures = false;
    let world_model = Arc::new(WorldModel::new());

    for task_id in order {
        let node = graph.nodes.get(&task_id).unwrap().clone();
        let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[SCHEDULER] Dispatching DAG Node: {}\nvia Subagent: {}", node.objective, node.required_agent)}).to_string());

        let mut task_context_history = String::new();
        for dep in &node.deps {
            if let Some(out) = task_outputs.get(dep) {
                let bounded_out = if out.len() > 1500 {
                    format!("{}... [TRUNCATED FOR CONTEXT BOUNDS]", &out[..1500])
                } else {
                    out.clone()
                };
                task_context_history.push_str(&format!(
                    "---\n[DEPENDENCY FOCUS FROM {}]:\n{}\n---\n",
                    dep, bounded_out
                ));
            }
        }

        let combined_mission = format!(
            "OBJECTIVE: {}\nASSIGNED ROLE: {}\n\nLINEAGE CONTEXT:\n{}",
            node.objective, node.required_agent, task_context_history
        );

        let mut loop_cortex = CorticalLoop::new(
            ctx.api_key.clone(),
            world_model.clone(),
            ctx.memory.clone(),
            ctx.vmo_state.clone(),
            ctx.dist.clone(),
            ctx.tx.clone(),
        );

        let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX] Igniting Epistemic Session for Node {}", task_id)}).to_string());

        let execution_evidence = match loop_cortex.ignite(&combined_mission).await {
            Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief, evidence }) => {
                format!(
                    "[DAG NODE COMPLETE] Objective: {}\nBelief: {:.2}\nEvidence:\n{}",
                    node.objective, belief, evidence
                )
            }
            Ok(CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted { best_belief, evidence }) => {
                mission_had_failures = true;
                let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX INCOMPLETE] Node {} exhausted turns (best belief {:.2}).", task_id, best_belief)}).to_string());
                format!("[DAG NODE INCOMPLETE] Objective: {}\nBest belief: {:.2}\nPartial evidence:\n{}", node.objective, best_belief, evidence)
            }
            Err(e) => {
                mission_had_failures = true;
                let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[CORTEX FRACTURE] Node {} loop failed: {}", task_id, e)}).to_string());
                format!("[DAG NODE FAILED] Ancestor node '{}' failed: {}", node.objective, e)
            }
        };

        task_outputs.insert(task_id.clone(), execution_evidence.clone());

        // ⚡ FIX: Immediate Hard Abort if a dependency fails or runs out of turns
        if mission_had_failures || execution_evidence.contains("[CORTEX INCOMPLETE]") || execution_evidence.contains("[DAG NODE FAILED]") || execution_evidence.contains("[DAG NODE INCOMPLETE]") {
            let _ = ctx.tx.send(serde_json::json!({"type": "LOG_EVENT", "payload": format!("[DAG HALT] Aborting downstream execution. Dependency '{}' failed to complete.", task_id)}).to_string());
            break;
        }
    }

    if mission_had_failures {
        format!("DAG/GoT Execution FINISHED WITH INCOMPLETE NODES. Plan ID: {}", plan_id)
    } else {
        format!("DAG/GoT Hybrid Execution Complete. Plan ID: {}", plan_id)
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/runtime_core/src/cortical_loop.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{sleep, Duration};

use crate::action_gate::ActionGate;
use crate::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use crate::cognition::epistemic_session::EpistemicSession;
use crate::cognition_graph::{EdgeRelation, NodeKind};
use crate::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use crate::epistemic_engine::EpistemicEngine;
use crate::executor::RoboticExecutor;
use crate::intent_contract::IntentContract;
use crate::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use crate::motor_cortex::MotorCortex;
use crate::policy::Capability;
use crate::shared_state::VmoState;
use crate::world_model::WorldModel;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum CortexOutcome {
    Proven { belief: f32, evidence: String },
    TurnsExhausted { best_belief: f32, evidence: String },
}

pub struct CorticalLoop {
    client: Client,
    api_key: String,
    world_model: Arc<WorldModel>,
    memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    action_gate: ActionGate,
}

impl CorticalLoop {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: String,
        world_model: Arc<WorldModel>,
        memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
        vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
        dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
        tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            client: Client::new(),
            api_key,
            world_model,
            memory,
            vmo_state,
            dist,
            tx,
            action_gate: ActionGate::new(),
        }
    }

    fn log(&self, msg: String) {
        let _ = self.tx.send(
            serde_json::json!({"type": "NEURAL_STREAM", "payload": format!("{}\n", msg)})
                .to_string(),
        );
    }

    async fn resolve_workspace_paths() -> String {
        match tokio::process::Command::new("cargo")
            .args(["metadata", "--no-deps", "--format-version=1"])
            .output()
            .await
        {
            Ok(output) if output.status.success() => {
                match serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&output.stdout) {
                    Ok(meta) => {
                        let workspace_root = meta
                            .get("workspace_root")
                            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                            .unwrap_or("<unresolved>");
                        let target_dir = meta
                            .get("target_directory")
                            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                            .unwrap_or("<unresolved>");
                        format!(
                            "WORKSPACE FACTS (ground truth, do not guess): workspace_root={workspace_root} | target_dir={target_dir}."
                        )
                    }
                    Err(e) => format!("WORKSPACE FACTS: cargo metadata parse failed ({e})."),
                }
            }
            Ok(output) => format!("WORKSPACE FACTS: cargo metadata exited non-zero ({}).", output.status),
            Err(e) => format!("WORKSPACE FACTS: cargo metadata failed to run ({e})."),
        }
    }

    pub async fn ignite(&mut self, mission: &str) -> Result<CortexOutcome> {
        self.ignite_with_evidence(mission, None).await
    }

    pub async fn ignite_with_evidence(&mut self, mission: &str, ancestor_evidence: Option<&str>) -> Result<CortexOutcome> {
        self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] Igniting Epistemic Reasoning Session\x1b[0m".to_string());

        let mut context_chunks = (*self.world_model).semantic_query(mission).await;
        context_chunks.push(Self::resolve_workspace_paths().await);
        if let Some(evidence) = ancestor_evidence {
            if !evidence.trim().is_empty() {
                context_chunks.push(format!("\n[ANCESTOR NODE EVIDENCE]\n{}", evidence));
            }
        }
        let world_context = context_chunks.join("\n");

        let mut session = EpistemicSession::new(mission);

        self.log("\x1b[36m>> [CORTEX] Generating competing strategies...\x1b[0m".to_string());
        session.budget.record_call();
        let hypotheses = EpistemicEngine::generate_hypotheses(
            &self.client,
            &self.api_key,
            mission,
            &world_context,
        )
        .await?;

        for h in hypotheses {
            session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Hypothesis, &h, 0.5, 100);
            self.log(format!("\x1b[90m  -> Strategy: {}\x1b[0m", h));
        }

        let is_mutation_task = session.contract.is_mutation_task;
        let is_write_task = session.contract.objective.to_lowercase().contains("write") || session.contract.objective.to_lowercase().contains("export");

        for turn in 1..=session.budget.max_turns {
            self.log(format!("\n\x1b[1;33m>> [CORTEX] Cognitive Turn {} / {}\x1b[0m", turn, session.budget.max_turns));

            if let Some(best_node) = session.graph.select_best_hypothesis().cloned() {
                let h_id = best_node.id;
                let h_content = best_node.content.clone();
                let current_belief = best_node.belief_score;
                if current_belief > session.max_observed_belief {
                    session.max_observed_belief = current_belief;
                    session.best_hypothesis_id = Some(h_id);
                }
                self.log(format!(
                    "\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX] Exploring Top Strategy (Belief: {:.2}):\x1b[0m {}",
                    current_belief, h_content
                ));

                let evidence_history = session.collect_evidence(h_id);

                // ⚡ LOCAL DETERMINISTIC BACKSTOP
                if is_write_task && session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]")) {
                    self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] DETERMINISTIC GUARD: Write objective satisfied with local [WRITE_OK]. Halting Node successfully.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                    return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief: 1.0, evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id) });
                } else if is_mutation_task && session.is_subgoal_satisfied(current_belief) {
                    self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] DETERMINISTIC GUARD: Patch objective satisfied with verified mutation contract. Halting Node successfully.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                    return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief: 1.0, evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id) });
                } else if !is_mutation_task && !is_write_task && current_belief >= 0.85 && (session.local_evidence.contains("[READ_CHUNK_OK]") || session.local_evidence.contains("[READ_OK]")) {
                    self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] DETERMINISTIC GUARD: Search/Audit objective satisfied with valid read evidence. Halting Node successfully.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                    return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven {
                        belief: current_belief,
                        evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id),
                    });
                }

                // ⚡ STRICT BUDGET EXHAUSTION GUARD (PHASE 8C)
                if session.budget.is_exhausted(turn) {
                    self.log("\x1b[1;31m>> [SUBGOAL_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED] Cognitive call limit reached before contract satisfaction. Node failed.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                    return Ok(CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted {
                        best_belief: current_belief,
                        evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id),
                    });
                }

                self.log("\x1b[36m>> [STRATEGIST] Planning next maneuver...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                session.budget.record_call();
                let proposed_action = match EpistemicEngine::propose_action(
                    &self.client,
                    &self.api_key,
                    mission,
                    &h_content,
                    &world_context,
                    &evidence_history,
                )
                .await
                {
                    Ok(action) => action,
                    Err(e) => {
                        self.log(format!("\x1b[31m>> [STRATEGIST FAULT]\x1b[0m {}", e));
                        continue;
                    }
                };
                self.log(format!("\x1b[35m>> [STRATEGIST PROPOSAL]:\x1b[0m {}", proposed_action));

                // ⚡ DETERMINISTIC HALT SHORT-CIRCUIT
                let prop_lower = proposed_action.trim().to_lowercase();
                if prop_lower == "halt" || prop_lower.starts_with("halt ") {
                    let has_prior_evidence = if is_write_task {
                        session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]"))
                    } else if is_mutation_task {
                        session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]"))
                    } else {
                        session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_CHUNK_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[SEARCH_CODE_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[READ_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[WRITE_OK]"))
                            || session.local_evidence.lines().any(|l| l.starts_with("[CMD_OK]"))
                    };

                    let compound_satisfied = if is_mutation_task {
                        session.is_subgoal_satisfied(current_belief)
                    } else if is_write_task {
                        true
                    } else {
                        let mission_lower = mission.to_lowercase();
                        let objective_part = mission_lower
                            .lines()
                            .find(|line| line.starts_with("objective:"))
                            .unwrap_or(mission_lower.as_str());
                        let evidence_lower = format!(
                            "{} {}",
                            evidence_history.to_lowercase(),
                            session.local_evidence.to_lowercase()
                        );
                        let and_segments: Vec<&str> = objective_part
                            .split(" and ")
                            .map(|s| s.trim())
                            .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                            .collect();
                        and_segments.iter().all(|segment| {
                            let or_parts: Vec<&str> = segment
                                .split(" or ")
                                .map(|s| s.trim())
                                .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                                .collect();
                            if or_parts.len() <= 1 {
                                segment
                                    .split_whitespace()
                                    .filter(|w| w.len() > 3)
                                    .any(|w| evidence_lower.contains(w))
                            } else {
                                or_parts.iter().any(|part| {
                                    part.split_whitespace()
                                        .filter(|w| w.len() > 3)
                                        .any(|w| evidence_lower.contains(w))
                                })
                            }
                        })
                    };

                    if has_prior_evidence && compound_satisfied {
                        self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [CORTEX] DETERMINISTIC GUARD: Halt proposed following successful local evidence. Subtask complete.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                        return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven {
                            belief: 1.0,
                            evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id),
                        });
                    } else if has_prior_evidence && !compound_satisfied {
                        self.log("\x1b[1;33m>> [CORTEX] DETERMINISTIC GUARD SUPPRESSED: Halt proposed but evidence covers only part of a compound objective; continuing to explore.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                    } else if (is_mutation_task || is_write_task) && !has_prior_evidence {
                        self.log("\x1b[1;31m>> [CORTEX FAULT] Halt rejected: Objective requires patching/writing, but no mutation success marker found.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                        let obs_id = session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, "[HALT_REJECTED] The directive requires modifying files. You proposed halt without successfully patching anything. You MUST use diff_patch, patch_function, or write_file.", 1.0, 50);
                        session.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, 0.6);
                        session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                        continue;
                    }
                }

                // --- ANTI-LOOP KINETIC GUARD ---
                let action_key = proposed_action.trim().to_string();
                if session.is_action_attempted(&action_key) {
                    if !session.local_evidence.trim().is_empty() && current_belief >= 0.60 {
                        self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [SUBGOAL_COMPLETE] Repeated proposal detected with valid prior evidence.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                        return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven {
                            belief: current_belief,
                            evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id),
                        });
                    }
                    self.log(format!("\x1b[1;31m>> [ENTERPRISE CORTEX] Anti-Loop Guard Triggered: Action '{}' already attempted.\x1b[0m", action_key));
                    let obs_id = session.graph.add_node(
                        NodeKind::Observation,
                        "[LOOP_PREVENTED] You already proposed and executed this exact command. Stop repeating actions.",
                        1.0, 50
                    );
                    session.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, 0.40);
                    session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                    continue;
                }
                session.record_action_attempt(&action_key);

                self.log("\x1b[31m>> [RED TEAM] Criticizing proposal...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                session.budget.record_call();
                match EpistemicEngine::criticize_proposal(
                    &self.client,
                    &self.api_key,
                    mission,
                    &h_content,
                    &proposed_action,
                    &world_context,
                    &evidence_history,
                )
                .await
                {
                    Err(e) => {
                        self.log(format!("\x1b[31m>> [CORTEX FATAL] Red Team critic parser panicked: {}\x1b[0m", e));
                        let evidence = format!("[CRITIC_PARSE_FAILURE] Critic response could not be parsed: {}", e);
                        let obs_id = session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, &evidence, 1.0, 50);
                        session.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, 0.15);
                        session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                    }
                    Ok(critic) => {
                        self.log(format!(
                            "\x1b[31m>> [CRITIC] Verdict:\x1b[0m {} (Modifier: {:.2})",
                            critic.critique, critic.confidence_modifier
                        ));

                        if critic.confidence_modifier > -0.2 {
                            self.log("\x1b[36m>> [CORTEX] Translating intent to physical action...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                            session.budget.record_call();

                            let parsed_actions = if let Ok(direct_actions) = crate::json_parser::extract_actions(&proposed_action) {
                                if !direct_actions.is_empty() {
                                    self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Direct JSON Proposal Intercept: Bypassing Translator LLM.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                    Ok(direct_actions)
                                } else {
                                    if let Ok(raw_json) = EpistemicEngine::translate_to_action(&self.client, &self.api_key, &h_content, &proposed_action).await {
                                        crate::json_parser::extract_actions(&raw_json)
                                    } else {
                                        Ok(vec![])
                                    }
                                }
                            } else {
                                if let Ok(raw_json) = EpistemicEngine::translate_to_action(&self.client, &self.api_key, &h_content, &proposed_action).await {
                                    crate::json_parser::extract_actions(&raw_json)
                                } else {
                                    Ok(vec![])
                                }
                            };

                            if let Ok(mut actions) = parsed_actions {
                                if actions.is_empty() && !proposed_action.trim().is_empty() {
                                    let action_str = proposed_action.trim();
                                    if let Some(cmd) = action_str.strip_prefix("execute_command") {
                                        let clean_cmd = cmd.trim().trim_matches('\'').trim_matches('"').to_string();
                                        self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling execute_command directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        actions.push(AgentAction::ExecuteCommand {
                                            command: clean_cmd,
                                            reasoning: "Intercepted fallback execute_command".to_string(),
                                            confidence: 0.9,
                                        });
                                    } else if action_str.starts_with("search_code") {
                                        let parts: Vec<&str> = action_str.split_whitespace().collect();
                                        if parts.len() >= 3 {
                                            let path = parts[1].to_string();
                                            let query = parts[2..].join(" ").trim_matches('\'').trim_matches('"').to_string();
                                            self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling search_code directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            actions.push(AgentAction::SearchCode {
                                                path,
                                                query,
                                                reasoning: "Intercepted fallback search_code".to_string(),
                                            });
                                        }
                                    } else if action_str.starts_with("read_file_chunk") {
                                        let parts: Vec<&str> = action_str.split_whitespace().collect();
                                        if parts.len() >= 2 {
                                            let path = parts[1].to_string();
                                            let start_line = parts.get(2).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
                                            let max_lines = parts.get(3).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(500);
                                            self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling read_file_chunk directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            actions.push(AgentAction::ReadFileChunk {
                                                path,
                                                start_line,
                                                max_lines,
                                                reasoning: "Intercepted fallback read_file_chunk".to_string(),
                                            });
                                        }
                                    } else if let Some(stripped) = action_str.strip_prefix("diff_patch ") {
                                        let parts: Vec<&str> = stripped.split_whitespace().collect();
                                        if parts.len() >= 3 {
                                            let path = parts[0].to_string();
                                            let search = parts[1].to_string();
                                            let replace = parts[2..].join(" ");
                                            self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling diff_patch directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            actions.push(AgentAction::DiffPatch {
                                                path,
                                                search,
                                                replace,
                                                reasoning: "Intercepted fallback diff_patch".to_string(),
                                            });
                                        }
                                    } else if let Some(stripped) = action_str.strip_prefix("patch_function ") {
                                        let parts: Vec<&str> = stripped.split_whitespace().collect();
                                        if parts.len() >= 3 {
                                            let path = parts[0].to_string();
                                            let function_name = parts[1].to_string();
                                            let new_body = parts[2..].join(" ");
                                            self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling patch_function directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            actions.push(AgentAction::PatchFunction {
                                                path,
                                                function_name,
                                                new_body,
                                                reasoning: "Intercepted fallback patch_function".to_string(),
                                            });
                                        }
                                    } else if let Some(stripped) = action_str.strip_prefix("write_file ") {
                                        if let Some(space_idx) = stripped.find(' ') {
                                            let path = stripped[..space_idx].trim().to_string();
                                            let content = stripped[space_idx..].trim().to_string();
                                            self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling write_file directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                            actions.push(AgentAction::WriteFile {
                                                path,
                                                content,
                                                reasoning: "Intercepted fallback write_file".to_string(),
                                                confidence: 0.9,
                                            });
                                        }
                                    } else if let Some(path) = action_str.strip_prefix("read_file ") {
                                        self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling read_file directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        actions.push(AgentAction::ReadFile {
                                            path: path.trim().to_string(),
                                            reasoning: "Intercepted fallback read_file".to_string(),
                                        });
                                    } else if let Some(raw_query) = action_str.strip_prefix("search_memory") {
                                        let query = raw_query.trim().trim_matches('\'').trim_matches('"').to_string();
                                        self.log("\x1b[32m>> [CORTEX INTERCEPT] Native Tool Intercept: Handling search_memory directly.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        actions.push(AgentAction::SearchMemory {
                                            query,
                                            reasoning: "Intercepted fallback search_memory".to_string(),
                                        });
                                    } else {
                                        self.log("\x1b[33m>> [CORTEX FALLBACK] Translator produced empty JSON. Executing proposed command directly...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        actions.push(AgentAction::ExecuteCommand {
                                            command: proposed_action.clone(),
                                            reasoning: "Fallback raw command execution".to_string(),
                                            confidence: 0.8,
                                        });
                                    }
                                }

                                if actions.is_empty() {
                                    self.log("\x1b[31m>> [CORTEX FAULT] Translator produced an empty action list.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                    let obs_id = session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, "[TRANSLATION_EMPTY]", 1.0, 50);
                                    session.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, 0.15);
                                    session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                                } else {
                                    let mut executor = RoboticExecutor::new(self.api_key.clone(), self.tx.clone());

                                    for action in actions {
                                        let intent = IntentContract::new(&h_content, current_belief);
                                        let capability = match &action {
                                            AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { .. } => Capability::ExecuteSystemCall,
                                            AgentAction::WriteFile { .. } | AgentAction::DiffPatch { .. } | AgentAction::PatchFunction { .. } => Capability::WriteSubstrate,
                                            AgentAction::Halt { .. } => Capability::HaltCognition,
                                            _ => Capability::ReadSubstrate,
                                        };

                                        // Capture the mutation target path now -- `action` is moved
                                        // into MotorCortex::translate() below and won't be readable
                                        // after this point, but we need the path later to auto-revert
                                        // if the Evaluator scores this mutation negative.
                                        let mutation_path: Option<String> = match &action {
                                            AgentAction::WriteFile { path, .. }
                                            | AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, .. }
                                            | AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, .. } => Some(path.clone()),
                                            _ => None,
                                        };

                                        let motor_cmd = MotorCortex::translate(
                                            &intent,
                                            None,
                                            action,
                                            capability,
                                            h_content.clone(),
                                        );

                                        if let Err(gate_err) = self.action_gate.approve(&motor_cmd) {
                                            self.log(format!("\x1b[1;31m>> [ACTION GATE REJECTED]: {}\x1b[0m", gate_err));
                                            let obs_id = session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, &format!("[ACTION_GATE_BLOCKED] {}", gate_err), 1.0, 50);
                                            session.graph.add_edge(obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, 0.50);
                                            session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                                            continue;
                                        }

                                        self.log("\x1b[35m>> [MOTOR CORTEX] Emitting authorized MotorCommand to RoboticExecutor...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        let evidence = match executor
                                            .execute_motor_command(
                                                motor_cmd,
                                                self.memory.clone(),
                                                self.vmo_state.clone(),
                                                self.dist.clone(),
                                            )
                                            .await
                                        {
                                            Ok(ev) => ev,
                                            Err(e) => format!("[ACTION FAILED] OS rejected action: {}", e),
                                        };

                                        session.local_evidence.push_str(&evidence);
                                        session.local_evidence.push('\n');

                                        let preview = evidence.lines().take(5).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
                                        self.log(format!("\x1b[32m>> [EVIDENCE ACQUIRED]\x1b[0m\n{}", preview));

                                        self.log("\x1b[33m>> [CORTEX] Evaluating physical evidence against strategy...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                        let eval_evidence_context = format!("Current Action Output:\n{}\n\nPrior Evidence in Task:\n{}", evidence, evidence_history);
                                        session.budget.record_call();
                                        match EpistemicEngine::evaluate_evidence(
                                            &self.client,
                                            &self.api_key,
                                            &h_content,
                                            &proposed_action,
                                            &eval_evidence_context,
                                        )
                                        .await
                                        {
                                            Err(e) => {
                                                self.log(format!("\x1b[31m>> [CORTEX FATAL] Evaluator panicked: {}\x1b[0m", e));
                                            }
                                            Ok(eval) => {
                                                self.log(format!(
                                                    "\x1b[33m>> [EVALUATION]\x1b[0m {} (Modifier: {:.2})",
                                                    eval.critique, eval.confidence_modifier
                                                ));

                                                session.record_evidence(&evidence, &eval, h_id);

                                                // AUTO-REVERT: a mutation the Evaluator scored negative must
                                                // not be allowed to stand as satisfied evidence. Revert the
                                                // file on disk and strip the raw marker from local_evidence so
                                                // completion contracts correctly see this as unresolved instead
                                                // of "patch succeeded technically" being treated as "objective
                                                // accomplished."
                                                if eval.confidence_modifier < 0.0 {
                                                    if let Some(path) = &mutation_path {
                                                        self.log(format!("\x1b[1;31m>> [CORTEX SAFETY] Mutation to '{}' scored negative ({:.2}) -- reverting via git checkout.\x1b[0m", path, eval.confidence_modifier));
                                                        let _ = crate::podman::PodmanShield::execute(&format!("git checkout -- {}", path));
                                                        session.local_evidence = session.local_evidence.replace(&evidence, "");
                                                        let reverted_tag = format!(
                                                            "[MUTATION_REVERTED] {} -- Evaluator rejected this mutation ({:.2}): {}\n",
                                                            path, eval.confidence_modifier, eval.critique
                                                        );
                                                        session.local_evidence.push_str(&reverted_tag);
                                                    }
                                                }

                                                if let Some(updated_node) = session.graph.nodes.get(&h_id) {
                                                    self.log(format!("\x1b[1;32m>> [CORTEX] Strategy Belief updated to {:.2}\x1b[0m", updated_node.belief_score));

                                                    if session.is_subgoal_satisfied(updated_node.belief_score) {
                                                        self.log("\x1b[1;36m>> [SUBGOAL_COMPLETE] Subgoal contract satisfied. Returning control.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                                                        return Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven {
                                                            belief: 1.0,
                                                            evidence: session.collect_evidence(h_id),
                                                        });
                                                    }
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            self.log("\x1b[31m>> [RED TEAM] VETO. Proposal rejected.\x1b[0m".to_string());
                            let veto_msg = format!("[RED TEAM VETO] Action blocked. Critic feedback: {}", critic.critique);
                            let veto_obs_id = session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Observation, &veto_msg, 1.0, 50);
                            session.graph.add_edge(veto_obs_id, h_id, EdgeRelation::Contradicts, critic.confidence_modifier.abs());
                            session.graph.propagate_beliefs();
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                self.log("\x1b[1;35m>> [CORTEX] All strategies exhausted. Re-orienting graph...\x1b[0m".to_string());
                let mut reorient_context = world_context.clone();
                if !session.executed_actions.is_empty() {
                    reorient_context.push_str("\n[ALREADY ATTEMPTED ACTIONS -- DO NOT REPEAT]\n");
                    for a in &session.executed_actions {
                        reorient_context.push_str(&format!("- {}\n", a));
                    }
                }
                session.budget.record_call();
                let new_hypotheses = EpistemicEngine::generate_hypotheses(
                    &self.client,
                    &self.api_key,
                    mission,
                    &reorient_context,
                )
                .await;
                for h in new_hypotheses.unwrap_or_default() {
                    session.graph.add_node(NodeKind::Hypothesis, &h, 0.5, 100);
                    self.log(format!("\x1b[90m  -> Strategy: {}\x1b[0m", h));
                }
            }
            sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
        }
        self.log("\x1b[1;32m>> [CORTEX] Epistemic Session Complete.\x1b[0m".to_string());
        let final_evidence = session.best_hypothesis_id
            .map(|id| session.collect_evidence(id))
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "No evidence gathered.".to_string());
        
        if is_mutation_task && !session.local_evidence.contains("[DIFF_PATCH_OK]") && !session.local_evidence.contains("[PATCH_FUNCTION_OK]") && !session.local_evidence.contains("[WRITE_OK]") {
            self.log("\x1b[1;31m>> [CORTEX FAULT] Task exhausted budget without satisfying mutation constraints.\x1b[0m".to_string());
            return Ok(CortexOutcome::TurnsExhausted { best_belief: 0.0, evidence: final_evidence });
        }

        Ok(CortexOutcome::Proven { belief: session.max_observed_belief.max(0.70), evidence: final_evidence })
    }
}

#![cfg(feature = "cognition_v5")]

use nexus_runtime_core::cognition::traits::*;
use nexus_runtime_core::cognition::reflection_engine::ReflectionEngine;
use nexus_runtime_core::cognition::strategy_manager::StrategyManager;
use nexus_runtime_core::cognition::prediction_engine::PredictionEngine;
use nexus_runtime_core::cognition::event_bus::EventBus;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[tokio::test]
async fn test_reflection_loop_success() {
    let bus = EventBus::new();
    let engine = ReflectionEngine::new(bus);
    
    let outcome = MissionOutcome {
        mission_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
        success: true,
        evidence_summary: "Execution completed perfectly.".into(),
        failures: vec![],
        successful_strategies: vec!["diff_patch".into()],
        raw_telemetry: "".into(),
        failed_strategy_id: None,
    };
    
    let report = engine.reflect(&outcome).await.expect("Reflection failed");
    
    assert!(report.confidence > 0.5, "Confidence should be high on success");
    assert!(!report.memory_updates.is_empty(), "Successful missions should yield memory updates");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn test_failure_learning() {
    let bus = EventBus::new();
    let engine = ReflectionEngine::new(bus);
    let strat_id = Uuid::new_v4();
    
    let outcome = MissionOutcome {
        mission_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
        success: false,
        evidence_summary: "Execution failed due to OS rejection.".into(),
        failures: vec!["timeout".into()],
        successful_strategies: vec![],
        raw_telemetry: "".into(),
        failed_strategy_id: Some(strat_id),
    };
    
    let report = engine.reflect(&outcome).await.expect("Reflection failed");
    
    assert_eq!(report.belief_updates.len(), 1, "Should generate exactly 1 belief update");
    assert_eq!(report.belief_updates[0].target, strat_id, "Belief update should target the failed strategy");
    assert!(report.belief_updates[0].delta < 0.0, "Delta should be negative for failure");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn test_prediction_error() {
    let engine = PredictionEngine::new();
    let action_id = Uuid::new_v4();
    
    let pred = engine.generate_prediction(action_id, "file_written", 0.1);
    engine.record_prediction(pred);

    let eval_correct = engine.evaluate_prediction(action_id, "Output: file_written successfully");
    assert_eq!(eval_correct.accuracy, 1.0, "Accuracy should be 1.0 for matching prediction");
    assert_eq!(eval_correct.error, 0.0, "Error should be 0.0 for matching prediction");

    let eval_incorrect = engine.evaluate_prediction(action_id, "Output: permission denied");
    assert_eq!(eval_incorrect.accuracy, 0.0, "Accuracy should be 0.0 for failed prediction");
    assert_eq!(eval_incorrect.error, 1.0, "Error should be 1.0 for failed prediction");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn test_strategy_adaptation() {
    let engine = StrategyManager::new();
    let goal = Goal {
        id: Uuid::new_v4(),
        title: "Test Goal".into(),
        description: "".into(),
        priority: 0.5,
        objectives: vec![],
        status: GoalStatus::Pending,
    };

    let ctx_success = StrategyContext {
        goal: goal.clone(),
        history: vec![],
        active_beliefs: Default::default(),
        available_resources: 1.0,
        mission_type: "Test".into(),
    };
    let strat_success = engine.select_strategy(&ctx_success).await.expect("Strategy selection failed");
    assert!(strat_success.contains("Standard exploratory execution"), "Should choose standard exploration on clean history");

    let failed_outcome = MissionOutcome {
        mission_id: Uuid::new_v4(),
        success: false,
        evidence_summary: "".into(),
        failures: vec!["error".into()],
        successful_strategies: vec![],
        raw_telemetry: "".into(),
        failed_strategy_id: None,
    };
    let ctx_failure = StrategyContext {
        goal: goal.clone(),
        history: vec![failed_outcome],
        active_beliefs: Default::default(),
        available_resources: 1.0,
        mission_type: "Test".into(),
    };
    let strat_failure = engine.select_strategy(&ctx_failure).await.expect("Strategy selection failed");
    assert!(strat_failure.contains("Conservative fallback"), "Should choose fallback on failure history");
}

use crate::genome::Genome;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

pub struct PromptCompiler;

impl PromptCompiler {
    /// Dynamically scans the apps/ directory to build a live whitelist of authorized tools.
    fn get_live_toolchain_whitelist() -> String {
        let mut tools = Vec::new();
        // The Daemon runs from the workspace root (AthenaFusionX/)
        let apps_dir = Path::new("apps");
        
        if let Ok(entries) = fs::read_dir(apps_dir) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                if let Ok(file_type) = entry.file_type() {
                    // Crate names are the directory names in apps/
                    if file_type.is_dir() {
                        tools.push(entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().to_string());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        
        if tools.is_empty() {
            return "No native tools currently registered.".to_string();
        }
        
        tools.join(", ")
    }

    pub fn compile_planner_prompt(genome: &Genome) -> String {
        let live_tools = Self::get_live_toolchain_whitelist();

        format!(
            "SYSTEM_POLICY: [PLANNER]\nStyle: {:?}\nDecomposition: {}\nBranching: {}\nRisk Threshold: {:.2}\n\n\
            STRICT CONSTRAINTS: Follow the defined architectural patterns.\n\n\
            AVAILABLE TOOLCHAIN:\n\
            [{}]\n\n\
            TOOL INSTRUCTION: AST PATCHER\n\
            When you need to mutate or rewrite Rust code, NEVER use standard sed, awk, or diff. YOU MUST use the semantic AST Patcher.\n\
            1. Write your completely new, valid Rust function or impl block into a temporary file (e.g., /tmp/mutation.rs).\n\
            2. Invoke the tool exactly like this:\n\
               ./target/release/ast_patcher --target <path/to/file.rs> --symbol <function_name> --patch-file /tmp/mutation.rs\n\
            3. The tool will safely parse the AST, replace the target symbol, and auto-format. Do not worry about exact line numbers.",
            genome.genes.planning.style,
            genome.genes.planning.decomposition_depth,
            genome.genes.planning.branch_factor,
            genome.genes.risk_threshold,
            live_tools
        )
    }

    pub fn compile_critic_prompt(genome: &Genome) -> String {
        let live_tools = Self::get_live_toolchain_whitelist();

        format!(
            "SYSTEM_POLICY: [CRITIC]\nStyle: {:?}\nVeto Threshold: {:.2}\nEvidence Weight: {:.2}\n\n\
            STRICT CONSTRAINTS: Evaluate without bias according to strict metrics.\n\n\
            CRITICAL RULE 4: TOOLCHAIN TRUST & EXPLORATORY GRACE\n\
            The following native tools are currently compiled, registered, and PRE-AUTHORIZED for execution:\n\
            [{}]\n\
            If the Strategist proposes executing ANY of these tools via target/release/ or cargo run, you MUST ASSUME the tool exists and is authorized. Do NOT veto execution on the basis of missing prerequisite files (like trace logs or candidate JSONs). Allow exploratory tool execution.",
            genome.genes.critic.style,
            genome.genes.critic.veto_threshold,
            genome.genes.critic.evidence_weight,
            live_tools
        )
    }
}

use crate::genome::{
    CognitiveGenes, CriticGenes, CriticStyle, Genome, GenomeMetadata, PlanningGenes,
    PlanningStyle, ToolBias,
};
use crate::repository::GenomeRepository;
use chrono::Utc;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct EvolutionDirector {
    pub repository: GenomeRepository,
}

impl EvolutionDirector {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Bootstraps Generation 0 if no active genome exists.
    pub fn boot_genesis_genome(&mut self) -> &Genome {
        if self.repository.active.is_none() {
            let genesis_id = Uuid::new_v4();
            
            let genesis_genome = Genome {
                id: genesis_id,
                metadata: GenomeMetadata {
                    generation: 0,
                    parent_ids: vec![],
                    mutation_reason: "SYSTEM_INIT: Baseline Genesis Genome".to_string(),
                    created_at: Utc::now(),
                },
                genes: CognitiveGenes {
                    planning: PlanningGenes {
                        style: PlanningStyle::GraphOfThoughts,
                        decomposition_depth: 3,
                        branch_factor: 2,
                    },
                    critic: CriticGenes {
                        style: CriticStyle::Adversarial,
                        veto_threshold: 0.85,
                        evidence_weight: 1.0,
                    },
                    risk_threshold: 0.20,
                    tool_bias: ToolBias::Conservative,
                },
            };

            self.repository.save_genome(genesis_genome);
            self.repository.active = Some(genesis_id);
        }

        self.repository.get_active_genome().unwrap()
    }

    pub fn analyze_evidence(&self, _evidence: &str) -> Option<Genome> {
        // Future implementation: Evaluate fitness and trigger mutation
        None
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: crates/evolution_engine/src/fitness_evaluator.rs 
=========================================
use crate::genome::SystemGenome;
use anyhow::Result;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FitnessScore {
    pub score: f32,
    pub execution_time_ms: u64,
    pub token_efficiency: f32,
}

pub struct FitnessEvaluator;

impl FitnessEvaluator {
    pub async fn evaluate(
        genome: &SystemGenome,
        benchmark_latency_ms: u64,
        tokens_used: u32,
    ) -> Result<FitnessScore> {
        let time_penalty = (benchmark_latency_ms as f32) * 0.01;
        let token_penalty = (tokens_used as f32) * 0.005;
        
        let base_score = 100.0 + (genome.weights.heuristic_reward * 50.0);
        let final_score = (base_score - time_penalty - token_penalty).max(0.0);

        Ok(FitnessScore {
            score: final_score,
            execution_time_ms: benchmark_latency_ms,
            token_efficiency: if tokens_used > 0 { 1000.0 / (tokens_used as f32) } else { 0.0 },
        })
    }
}

use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct CognitiveWeights {
    pub meta_reasoning_threshold: f32, // When to trigger GoT
    pub critic_penalty_weight: f32,    // How heavily to penalize vetoes
    pub heuristic_reward: f32,         // Reward for physical exploration
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct SystemGenome {
    pub generation: u32,
    pub hash: String,
    pub weights: CognitiveWeights,
    pub active_wasm_modules: Vec<String>,
    pub primary_heavy_model: String,
    pub primary_light_model: String,
    pub fitness_score: f32,
}

impl SystemGenome {
    pub fn baseline() -> Self {
        Self {
            generation: 0,
            hash: "genesis_0000".to_string(),
            weights: CognitiveWeights {
                meta_reasoning_threshold: 0.60,
                critic_penalty_weight: -0.30,
                heuristic_reward: 0.30,
            },
            active_wasm_modules: vec![],
            primary_heavy_model: "gemma-4-31b-it".to_string(),
            primary_light_model: "gemini-3.1-flash-lite".to_string(),
            fitness_score: 100.0,
        }
    }
}

FILE: crates/evolution_engine/src/mutator.rs 
=========================================
use crate::genome::SystemGenome;
use rand::Rng;

pub struct Mutator;

impl Mutator {
    pub fn mutate(parent: &SystemGenome) -> SystemGenome {
        let mut rng = rand::thread_rng();
        let mut child = parent.clone();
        
        child.generation += 1;
        
        // Drift cognitive weights by +/- 10%
        let mut drift = || rng.gen_range(0.90..1.10);
        
        child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold *= drift();
        child.weights.critic_penalty_weight *= drift();
        child.weights.heuristic_reward *= drift();

        // Enforce boundary constraints
        child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold = child.weights.meta_reasoning_threshold.clamp(0.1, 0.9);
        child.weights.heuristic_reward = child.weights.heuristic_reward.clamp(0.1, 0.8);

        // Generate deterministic child hash
        child.hash = format!("gen_{}_{}", child.generation, hex::encode(rng.gen::<[u8; 4]>()));
        
        child
    }
}

use crate::genome::{FitnessVector, Genome};
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use uuid::Uuid;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct GenomeEvaluation {
    pub benchmark_id: Uuid,
    pub fitness: FitnessVector,
    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct GenomeRepository {
    pub active: Option<Uuid>,
    pub lineage: HashMap<Uuid, Genome>,
    pub evaluations: HashMap<Uuid, Vec<GenomeEvaluation>>,
}

impl GenomeRepository {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    pub fn save_genome(&mut self, genome: Genome) {
        self.lineage.insert(genome.id, genome);
    }

    pub fn get_active_genome(&self) -> Option<&Genome> {
        self.active.and_then(|id| self.lineage.get(&id))
    }
}
