use futures_util::StreamExt;
use reqwest::Client;
use std::io::{self, Write};
use std::process::Command;
use tokio_tungstenite::connect_async;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[1;36m>> NEXUS SINGULARITY v4.0 — THIN CLIENT ONLINE\x1b[0m");
    println!("\x1b[36m==================================================\x1b[0m");

    let token = std::fs::read_to_string(".nexus_ui_token")
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "".to_string())
        .trim()
        .to_string();

    let ws_url = "ws://127.0.0.1:3000/ws/telemetry";

    let ws_stream = match connect_async(ws_url).await {
        Ok((stream, _)) => Some(stream),
        Err(_) => {
            println!("\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Could not connect to Daemon. Is it running?\x1b[0m");
            None
        }
    };

    if let Some(mut stream) = ws_stream {
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut last_type = String::new();
            while let Some(Ok(msg)) = stream.next().await {
                if let tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::Message::Text(text) = msg {
                    if let Ok(parsed) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) {
                        let msg_type = parsed["type"].as_str().unwrap_or("LOG");
                        let payload = parsed["payload"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
                        match msg_type {
                            "NEURAL_THOUGHT" | "VITALS" | "HEARTBEAT" => {}
                            "NEURAL_STREAM" => {
                                if last_type != "NEURAL_STREAM" {
                                    print!(
                                        "\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;36m[CORTEX EXECUTING]\x1b[0m\n\x1b[36m"
                                    );
                                }
                                print!("{}", payload);
                                let _ = io::stdout().flush();
                                last_type = "NEURAL_STREAM".to_string();
                            }
                            "TASK_STATUS" => {
                                println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;33m[SYS]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                                last_type = "TASK_STATUS".to_string();
                            }
                            "LOG_EVENT" => {
                                println!("\x1b[0m\n\x1b[1;90m[EVENT]\x1b[0m {}", payload);
                                last_type = "LOG_EVENT".to_string();
                            }
                            _ => {}
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });
    }

    println!("\x1b[32m>> Neural Uplink Established. Substrate Ready.\x1b[0m");
    let client = Client::new();
    let stdin = io::stdin();

    loop {
        print!("\n\x1b[1;32mnexus>\x1b[0m (Type prompt, then 'EOF' on a new line to submit)\n> ");
        io::stdout().flush()?;

        let mut input = String::new();
        loop {
            let mut line = String::new();
            let bytes_read = stdin.read_line(&mut line)?;
            if bytes_read == 0 || line.trim() == "EOF" {
                break;
            }
            input.push_str(&line);
        }

        let directive = input.trim();
        if directive.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        if directive == "/exit" || directive == "/quit" {
            break;
        }

        if directive.starts_with("/mutate ") {
            let rest = directive.strip_prefix("/mutate ").unwrap().trim();
            let mut parts = rest.splitn(2, |c: char| c == '\n' || c.is_whitespace());
            let target_func = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim();
            let instruction_raw = parts.next().unwrap_or("").trim();
            let user_instruction = if instruction_raw.is_empty() {
                "Analyze what it does, optimize or expand its capabilities"
            } else {
                instruction_raw
            };

            println!(
                "\x1b[33m>> [AST] Locating '{}' in repository_analysis/symbol_graph.json...\x1b[0m",
                target_func
            );

            // 1. Run the Impact Engine silently
            println!("\x1b[35m>> [IMPACT_ENGINE] Calculating Blast Radius...\x1b[0m");
            let _ = Command::new("cargo")
                .args(["run", "--bin", "impact_engine", "--", target_func])
                .output();

            let mut blast_radius_info = String::new();
            if let Ok(impact_data) =
                std::fs::read_to_string("repository_analysis/impact/latest_report.json")
            {
                if let Ok(impact) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&impact_data) {
                    if let Some(nodes) = impact["affected_nodes"].as_array() {
                        let names: Vec<&str> = nodes.iter().filter_map(|n| n.as_str()).collect();
                        blast_radius_info = format!(
                            "WARNING: This mutation has a blast radius of {}. You must preserve contracts with affected nodes: {}.",
                            impact["blast_radius"],
                            names.join(", ")
                        );
                        println!("\x1b[31m>> [IMPACT_ENGINE] {} \x1b[0m", blast_radius_info);
                    }
                }
            }

            if let Ok(map_data) = std::fs::read_to_string("repository_analysis/symbol_graph.json") {
                if let Ok(map) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&map_data) {
                    let mut found = false;
                    let target_suffix = format!("::{}", target_func);
                    let empty_vec = vec![];
                    let nodes = map
                        .get("nodes")
                        .and_then(|n| n.as_array())
                        .unwrap_or_else(|| map.as_array().unwrap_or(&empty_vec));

                    for node in nodes {
                        let id = node.get("id").and_then(|i| i.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                        let name = node.get("name").and_then(|n| n.as_str()).unwrap_or("");
                        if id.ends_with(&target_suffix) || name == target_func {
                            let path = node
                                .get("file_path")
                                .or_else(|| node.get("path"))
                                .and_then(|p| p.as_str())
                                .unwrap_or("");
                            let start =
                                node.get("start_line").and_then(|s| s.as_u64()).unwrap_or(1);
                            let end = node.get("end_line").and_then(|e| e.as_u64()).unwrap_or(50);
                            let ntype = node
                                .get("kind")
                                .or_else(|| node.get("node_type"))
                                .and_then(|k| k.as_str())
                                .unwrap_or("Function");

                            println!(
                                "\x1b[32m>> [AST] Found {} in {} (Lines {}-{})\x1b[0m",
                                ntype, path, start, end
                            );

                            let prompt = format!(
                                "MISSION UPDATE (GOD MODE):\n1. You are optimizing the {} `{}`.\n2. DO NOT read any files. The code is located in `{}` exactly from line {} to {}.\n3. IMPACT FORESIGHT: {}\n4. INSTRUCTION: {}\n5. Immediately use your `DiffPatch` tool to apply the improvements.\n6. Once patched, run `cargo check` to verify.",
                                ntype, target_func, path, start, end, blast_radius_info, user_instruction
                            );

                            println!("\x1b[36m>> [ORCHESTRATOR] Fusing Surgical Prompt with Impact Foresight...\x1b[0m");
                            let _res = client
                                .post("http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/task")
                                .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token))
                                .json(&serde_json::json!({ "directive": prompt }))
                                .send()
                                .await;

                            found = true;
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    if !found {
                        println!(
                            "\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Target '{}' not found in AST map.\x1b[0m",
                            target_func
                        );
                    }
                    continue;
                }
            }
        }

        // Default routing for non-/mutate commands
        let _res = client
            .post("http://127.0.0.1:3000/api/task")
            .header("Authorization", format!("Bearer {}", token))
            .json(&serde_json::json!({ "directive": directive }))
            .send()
            .await;

        match _res {
            Ok(response) => {
                if !response.status().is_success() {
                    println!(
                        "\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Daemon rejected task: {}\x1b[0m",
                        response.status()
                    );
                } else {
                    println!(
                        "\x1b[32m>> [OK] Task dispatched to Background Executive Cortex.\x1b[0m"
                    );
                }
            }
            Err(e) => println!("\x1b[31m>> [ERROR] Failed to contact Daemon: {}\x1b[0m", e),
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/scheduler.rs

use uuid::Uuid;

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum TaskState {
    Ready,
    Blocked,
    Suspended,
    Completed,
    Failed,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct ResourceBudget {
    pub cpu_budget_ms: u32,
    pub token_budget: usize,
    pub max_memory_bytes: usize,
    pub io_weight: u8,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RuntimeCounters {
    pub cpu_time_used_ms: u32,
    pub tokens_consumed: usize,
    pub memory_used_bytes: usize,
    pub io_operations: u64,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CognitiveTask {
    pub id: Uuid,
    pub lineage_id: Uuid,
    pub parent_lineage_id: Option<Uuid>,
    pub execution_class: ExecutionClass,
    pub state: TaskState,
    pub static_priority: u8,
    pub dynamic_priority: u32,
    pub budget: ResourceBudget,
    pub counters: RuntimeCounters,
    pub created_at: u64,
}

#[allow(dead_code)]
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub enum ExecutionClass {
    RealTimeTelemetry,
    SovereignInference,
    BackgroundConsolidation,
}

pub struct DeterministicScheduler {
    pub queue: Vec<CognitiveTask>,
}

impl DeterministicScheduler {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self { queue: Vec::new() }
    }

    pub fn submit(&mut self, task: CognitiveTask) {
        self.queue.push(task);
    }

    #[allow(dead_code)]
    pub fn dispatch(&mut self) -> Option<CognitiveTask> {
        if self.queue.is_empty() {
            None
        } else {
            Some(self.queue.remove(0))
        }
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/ws_bridge.rs

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{
    extract::ws::{Message, WebSocket, WebSocketUpgrade},
    extract::State,
    response::Response,
};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn ws_telemetry_handler(
    ws: WebSocketUpgrade,
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
) -> Response {
    ws.on_upgrade(move |socket| handle_socket(socket, state))
}

async fn handle_socket(mut socket: WebSocket, state: Arc<AppState>) {
    let mut rx = state.tx.subscribe();
    while let Ok(msg) = rx.recv().await {
        if socket.send(Message::Text(msg)).await.is_err() {
            break;
        }
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/security.rs

use crate::auth;
use axum::{
    body::Body,
    http::{header, Request, StatusCode},
    middleware::Next,
    response::Response,
};

pub async fn sovereign_auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: Next) -> Result<Response, StatusCode> {
    if req.uri().path() == "/api/auth/login" {
        return Ok(next.run(req).await);
    }

    let auth_header =
        req.headers().get(header::AUTHORIZATION).and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok()).unwrap_or("");
    let token = auth_header.trim_start_matches("Bearer ").trim();

    if !token.is_empty() {
        match auth::verify_token_detailed(token) {
            Ok(auth::TokenVerdict::Valid) => return Ok(next.run(req).await),
            Ok(verdict) => tracing::warn!(
                "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | Verdict={:?}",
                req.uri().path(),
                verdict
            ),
            Err(e) => tracing::error!(
                "[SECURITY] Auth key misconfiguration at {}: {}",
                req.uri().path(),
                e
            ),
        }
    } else {
        tracing::warn!(
            "[SECURITY] 401 Unauthorized: Path={} | No token provided",
            req.uri().path()
        );
    }
    Err(StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED)
}

pub fn write_startup_token() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    use std::io::Write;
    use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;

    let token = auth::issue_sovereign_token()?;
    // ⚡ FIX: Strict 0600 permissions
    std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
        .write(true)
        .create(true)
        .truncate(true)
        .mode(0o600)
        .open(".nexus_ui_token")?
        .write_all(token.as_bytes())?;

    println!("\x1b[32m>> [SECURITY] Startup token written to .nexus_ui_token (0600)\x1b[0m");
    Ok(())
}

=========================================
FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/auth.rs 
=========================================
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use axum::{http::StatusCode, response::IntoResponse, Json};
use jsonwebtoken::{decode, encode, Algorithm, DecodingKey, EncodingKey, Header, Validation};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use zeroize::Zeroizing;

const MIN_KEY_BYTES: usize = 32;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Claims {
    pub sub: String,
    pub exp: usize,
    pub iat: usize,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct LoginRequest {
    pub password: String,
}

pub async fn login_handler(Json(payload): Json<LoginRequest>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let secret = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY").unwrap_or_default();
    if payload.password == secret {
        match issue_sovereign_token() {
            Ok(token) => {
                (StatusCode::OK, Json(serde_json::json!({ "token": token }))).into_response()
            }
            Err(_) => (
                StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": "TOKEN_GEN_FAIL" })),
            )
                .into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, Json(serde_json::json!({ "error": "INVALID_CREDENTIALS" })))
            .into_response()
    }
}

pub fn load_key_at_startup() -> anyhow::Result<Zeroizing<String>> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow::anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set — refusing to start"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(),
            MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(Zeroizing::new(key))
}

const DEFAULT_TTL_SECS: u64 = 86_400;

fn load_key() -> Result<String> {
    let key = std::env::var("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY")
        .map_err(|_| anyhow!("NEXUS_AUTH_KEY env var is not set"))?;
    if key.len() < MIN_KEY_BYTES {
        return Err(anyhow!(
            "NEXUS_AUTH_KEY is too short ({} bytes). Minimum is {} bytes.",
            key.len(),
            MIN_KEY_BYTES
        ));
    }
    Ok(key)
}

fn token_ttl() -> u64 {
    match std::env::var("NEXUS_TOKEN_TTL_SECS") {
        Ok(v) => match v.parse::<u64>() {
            Ok(secs) => secs,
            Err(_) => DEFAULT_TTL_SECS,
        },
        Err(_) => DEFAULT_TTL_SECS,
    }
}

pub fn issue_sovereign_token() -> Result<String> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let now = SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)?.as_secs();
    let claims = Claims {
        sub: "architect".to_owned(),
        iat: now as usize,
        exp: (now + token_ttl()) as usize,
    };
    encode(&Header::default(), &claims, &EncodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()))
        .map_err(|e| anyhow!("JWT_GEN_FAIL: {}", e))
}

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum TokenVerdict {
    Valid,
    Expired,
    InvalidSignature,
    MissingKey,
}

pub fn verify_token_detailed(token: &str) -> Result<TokenVerdict> {
    let key = load_key()?;
    let validation = Validation::new(Algorithm::HS256);
    match decode::<Claims>(token, &DecodingKey::from_secret(key.as_bytes()), &validation) {
        Ok(_) => Ok(TokenVerdict::Valid),
        Err(e) => match *e.kind() {
            jsonwebtoken::errors::ErrorKind::ExpiredSignature => Ok(TokenVerdict::Expired),
            _ => Ok(TokenVerdict::InvalidSignature),
        },
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/broker.rs 
=========================================
//! Broker — deduplicates rapid fire directives within a 200 ms window
//! so the same button-mash doesn't flood the agent pipeline.
use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Instant;

static LAST_FIRE: Lazy<Mutex<HashMap<String, Instant>>> = Lazy::new(|| Mutex::new(HashMap::new()));

const DEBOUNCE_MS: u128 = 200;

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [BROKER] Directive deduplicator online");
}

/// Returns true when the directive should be forwarded (not a duplicate).
pub fn should_forward(key: &str) -> bool {
    let mut map = LAST_FIRE.lock().unwrap();
    let now = Instant::now();
    if let Some(last) = map.get(key) {
        if last.elapsed().as_millis() < DEBOUNCE_MS {
            return false;
        }
    }
    map.insert(key.to_string(), now);
    true
}

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{extract::State, Json};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn list_nodes(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let nodes = state.cluster.get_all_nodes();
    Json(json!({ "nodes": nodes }))
}

pub async fn cluster_status(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> Json<Value> {
    let best = state.cluster.best_node();
    Json(json!({
        "best_node": best,
        "has_capacity": best.is_some(),
    }))
}

/// WASM cell registry — returns cells that have been synthesised and stored.
/// Cells are written to .nexus_wasm_registry.json by wasm_forge when synthesised.
pub async fn list_wasm_cells(
    _state: axum::extract::State<std::sync::Arc<crate::server::AppState>>,
) -> axum::Json<serde_json::Value> {
    let path = ".nexus_wasm_registry.json";
    let cells: Vec<serde_json::Value> = if std::path::Path::new(path).exists() {
        tokio::fs::read_to_string(path)
            .await
            .ok()
            .and_then(|s| serde_json::from_str(&s).ok())
            .unwrap_or_default()
    } else {
        vec![]
    };
    axum::Json(serde_json::json!({ "cells": cells }))
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/dream_cycle.rs

use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, RwLock};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [DREAM_CYCLE] Subconscious synthesis engine initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    palace: Arc<RwLock<MemoryPalace>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    client: reqwest::Client,
    api_key: String,
    orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(30 * 60));
        ticker.tick().await;
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Beginning synthesis pass…");
            let _ = tx.send(
                serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT","payload":"[DREAM] Synthesis cycle awakening…"})
                    .to_string()
            );

            let strands = {
                let mem = palace.read().await;
                mem.fetch_random_strands(8).await.unwrap_or_default()
            };

            if strands.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            let context = strands
                .iter()
                .map(|s| format!("DIRECTIVE: {}\nRESULT: {}", s.directive, s.result))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n---\n");

            let prompt = format!(
                "You are NEXUS Archivist. Analyse these memory strands and write one concise \
                 architectural insight (3-5 sentences, plain text, no JSON):\n\n{}",
                context
            );

            let ctx = nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::ContextPacket {
                system_prompt: Some("Output plain text only. One paragraph.".into()),
                model_override: Some("gemma-4-26b-a4b-it".into()),
                ..Default::default()
            };

            match orchestrator.run(prompt, ctx, &client, &api_key, Some(tx.clone())).await {
                Ok(insight) => {
                    let mem = palace.read().await;
                    if let Err(e) = mem.store_insight(&insight).await {
                        tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Failed to store insight: {}", e);
                    } else {
                        tracing::info!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Insight stored");
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => tracing::warn!("[DREAM_CYCLE] Synthesis failed: {}", e),
            }
        }
    });
}

//! EdgeWorker — handles registration of remote cluster nodes via HTTP POST
//! to /api/cluster/register.  Routes are wired in server.rs.

use nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterNode;

    tracing::info!(">> [EDGE_WORKER] Peer registration handler online");
}

pub async fn register_node(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Json(node): Json<ClusterNode>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    state.cluster.register(node.clone());
    tracing::info!("[EDGE_WORKER] Node {} registered at {}:{}", node.id, node.host, node.port);

}

// FFI Telemetry Wrapper Module
// Intercepts process_cognitive_task return codes and maps them to OpenTelemetry span attributes.

pub struct FfiTelemetryWrapper;

impl FfiTelemetryWrapper {
    pub fn intercept_cognitive_task<F, R>(task_name: &str, f: F) -> R
    where
        F: FnOnce() -> (i32, R),
    {
        // Execute the underlying FFI call
        let (return_code, result) = f();
        
        // Map return code to error category and semantic attributes
        let error_category = match return_code {
            0 => "SUCCESS",
            1..=99 => "VALIDATION_ERROR",
            100..=199 => "INTERNAL_ERROR",
            _ => "UNKNOWN_ERROR",
        };

        // Emit via telemetry bridge pipeline if available
        // (Integrating with telemetry_bridge.rs structural logging)
        println!("[TelemetryBridge] span=process_cognitive_task task={} ffi.return_code={} ffi.error_category={}", 
            task_name, return_code, error_category);

        result
    }
}

pub trait ResourceAwareRouter {
    fn route_request(&self, model_size: usize, node_arch: &str) -> String {
        if node_arch == "arm64" && model_size > 1024 {
            "fallback_to_heavy_node".to_string()
        } else {
            "action_b_lightweight".to_string()
        }
    }
}

//! Heartbeat — sends a periodic JSON ping over the broadcast channel so
//! the WebSocket watchdog in socket.ts never fires a stale-connection close.
use serde_json::json;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [HEARTBEAT] Pulse initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(tx: broadcast::Sender<String>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(5));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let msg = json!({ "type": "HEARTBEAT", "ts": chrono_now() }).to_string();
            let _ = tx.send(msg);
        }
    });
}

fn chrono_now() -> u64 {
    std::time::SystemTime::now().duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH).unwrap_or_default().as_secs()
}

//! Logger — initialises tracing subscriber with env-filter and JSON output.
use tracing_subscriber::{fmt, EnvFilter};

pub fn init() {
    let filter = EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("nexus_ide_backend=info,nexus_runtime_core=info,warn"));

    fmt().with_env_filter(filter).with_target(true).with_thread_ids(false).compact().init();

    tracing::info!(">> [LOGGER] Tracing substrate online");
}

#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex, RwLock};

use nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh;
use nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace;
use secrecy::SecretString;

mod auth;
mod broker;
mod cluster_api;
mod dream_cycle;
mod edge_worker;
mod fs_watcher;
mod gateway_trait;
mod heartbeat;
mod logger;
mod scheduler;
mod security;
mod self_healing_engine;
mod server;
mod server_terminal;
mod telemetry_bridge;
mod thermal_governor;
mod vitals_handler;
mod ws_bridge;

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    logger::init();

    tracing::info!("==================================================");
    tracing::info!(">> NEXUS SINGULARITY v4.0 — DAEMON ONLINE");
    tracing::info!("==================================================");

    security::write_startup_token()?;
    let api_key_str = std::env::var("GEMINI_API_KEY").unwrap_or_default().trim().to_string();
    let api_key = SecretString::from(api_key_str.clone());
    let req_client = reqwest::Client::new();

    let (tx, _) = broadcast::channel::<String>(256);

    let palace = Arc::new(MemoryPalace::new("nexus_daemon.db").await?);

    // 👇 The crucial fix: We clone the palace Arc so it can be shared!
    let memory = Arc::new(MemoryCortex::new(palace.clone()));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(VmoState::default()));
    let cluster = Arc::new(ClusterMesh::new());
    let dist = Arc::new(DistributedScheduler::new(cluster.clone()));
    let orchestrator = Arc::new(Orchestrator::new());

    let monitor = Arc::new(Mutex::new(nexus_ide_backend::system_monitor::SystemMonitor::new()));
    let resonance = Arc::new(std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32::new(1000));

    let agent = Arc::new(nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent::init(tx.clone())?);
    let arbiter = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent::new(agent.clone()));

    // 🌟 Boot all Subconscious Background Threads 🌟
    dream_cycle::spawn(
        palace.clone(),
        tx.clone(),
        req_client.clone(),
        api_key_str.clone(),
        orchestrator.clone(),
    );
    self_healing_engine::spawn(
        orchestrator.clone(),
        req_client.clone(),
        api_key_str.clone(),
        memory.clone(),
        vmo_state.clone(),
        tx.clone(),
        dist.clone(),
        monitor.clone(),
    );
    telemetry_bridge::spawn(tx.clone(), monitor.clone(), resonance.clone());
    thermal_governor::spawn();
    heartbeat::spawn(tx.clone());

    let state = Arc::new(server::AppState {
        client: req_client,
        monitor,
        agent,
        orchestrator,
        memory,
        api_key,
        tx,
        arbiter,
        workspace_root: PathBuf::from("."),
        vmo_state,
        cluster,
        dist,
    });

    // 🌟 Boot WASM Sidecars 🌟
    let sidecars_dir = PathBuf::from("sidecars");
    if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(&sidecars_dir) {
        if let Ok(sidecar_host) = nexus_runtime_core::sidecar_host::SidecarHost::new(true) {
            let sidecar_host = Arc::new(sidecar_host);
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let path = entry.path();
                if path.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("wasm") {
                    tracing::info!(">> [SIDECAR] Loading external cognitive module: {:?}", path);
                    let host = sidecar_host.clone();
                    tokio::task::spawn_blocking(move || {
                        if let Err(e) = host.load_and_run(&path) {
                            let err_str = e.to_string();
                            if err_str.contains("_start")
                                || err_str.contains("failed to find function export")
                            {
                                tracing::debug!(
                                    "[SIDECAR] Loaded library module {:?} (no _start export)",
                                    path
                                );
                            } else {
                                tracing::error!("[SIDECAR] Failed to execute {:?}: {}", path, e);
                            }
                        }
                    });
                }
            }
        }
    }

    tracing::info!(">> Core Subsystems online. Binding Axum API Gateway to 0.0.0.0:3000...");
    server::start_server(state).await;

    Ok(())
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/self_healing_engine.rs 
=========================================
use nexus_ide_backend::system_monitor::SystemMonitor;
use nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler;
use nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [SELF_HEALING] Suture engine initialised");
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
pub fn spawn(
    _orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    _client: reqwest::Client,
    _api_key: String,
    _memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    _vmo_state: Arc<Mutex<VmoState>>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    _dist: Arc<DistributedScheduler>,
    monitor: Arc<Mutex<SystemMonitor>>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        // Ticks once a minute
        let mut ticker = tokio::time::interval(tokio::time::Duration::from_secs(60));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let vitals = {
                let mut m = monitor.lock().await;
                m.get_vitals()
            };

            // If RAM hits 85%, warn the user in the CLI UI
            if vitals.total_ram_gb > 0.0 && (vitals.ram_gb / vitals.total_ram_gb) > 0.85 {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[SELF_HEALING] High RAM usage: {:.2}GB. Throttling.",
                    vitals.ram_gb
                );
                let _ = tx.send(
                    serde_json::json!({"type":"LOG_EVENT","payload":"[SYS] High RAM usage. Self-healing throttled."}).to_string()
                );
            } else {
                // Otherwise, log quietly to daemon.log and do not pollute the CLI
                tracing::debug!("[SYS] Background Self-healing heartbeat active.");
            }
        }
    });
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/server.rs

use axum::{
    extract::{Query, State},
    http::{header, StatusCode, Uri},
    middleware,
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Json, Router,
};
use nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex;
use nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState;
use rust_embed::RustEmbed;
use secrecy::{ExposeSecret, SecretString};
use serde::Deserialize;
use serde_json::json;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::net::TcpListener;

use crate::{cluster_api, security, vitals_handler, ws_bridge};

// ⚡ FIX: Relative path from bins/ide_backend/Cargo.toml to the UI dist folder
#[derive(RustEmbed)]
#[folder = "../../ui/dist/"]
struct Assets;

pub struct AppState {
    pub client: reqwest::Client,
    pub monitor: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<nexus_ide_backend::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    pub agent: Arc<nexus_model_hub::neural_agent::MicroAgent>,
    pub orchestrator: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::orchestrator::Orchestrator>,
    pub memory: Arc<MemoryCortex>,
    pub api_key: SecretString,
    pub tx: tokio::sync::broadcast::Sender<String>,
    pub arbiter: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::arbiter_agent::ArbiterAgent>,
    pub workspace_root: PathBuf,
    pub vmo_state: Arc<tokio::sync::Mutex<VmoState>>,
    pub cluster: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh>,
    pub dist: Arc<nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler>,
}

#[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
pub struct Intent {
    pub directive: Option<String>,
}

pub async fn unified_gateway(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Json(intent): Json<Intent>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let directive_str = intent.directive.unwrap_or_default();
    let s = state.clone();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let d = nexus_runtime_core::cli_utils::inject_workspace_dna(&directive_str).await;
        nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::send_task(
            s.orchestrator.clone(),
            nexus_runtime_core::task_manager::TaskContext {
                client: s.client.clone(),
                api_key: s.api_key.expose_secret().to_string(),
                memory: s.memory.clone(),
                vmo_state: s.vmo_state.clone(),
                tx: s.tx.clone(),
                dist: s.dist.clone(),
            },
            &d,
        )
        .await;
    });
    (StatusCode::OK, Json(json!({ "status": "QUEUED" }))).into_response()
}

async fn metrics_handler() -> impl IntoResponse {
    let text = nexus_runtime_core::metrics::get_metrics_text();
    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/plain")], text)
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
pub struct FsQuery {
    path: Option<String>,
}

fn safe_path(
    workspace_root: &Path,
    user_path: &str,
) -> Result<PathBuf, (StatusCode, Json<serde_json::Value>)> {
    if user_path.is_empty() {
        return Err((StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_EMPTY" }))));
    }
    let path = std::path::Path::new(user_path);
    if path.is_absolute() || path.components().any(|c| matches!(c, std::path::Component::ParentDir))
    {
        return Err((StatusCode::FORBIDDEN, Json(json!({ "error": "PATH_TRAVERSAL_DENIED" }))));
    }
    Ok(workspace_root.join(path))
}

pub async fn fs_read_handler(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Query(q): Query<FsQuery>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&safe).await {
            Ok(content) => Json(json!({ "content": content })).into_response(),
            Err(e) => {
                (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() }))).into_response()
            }
        },
    }
}

pub async fn fs_write_handler(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Json(payload): Json<serde_json::Value>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = payload["path"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    let content = payload["content"].as_str().unwrap_or_default();
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            if let Some(parent) = safe.parent() {
                let _ = tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await;
            }
            match tokio::fs::write(&safe, content).await {
                Ok(_) => Json(json!({ "status": "success" })).into_response(),
                Err(e) => {
                    (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, Json(json!({ "error": e.to_string() })))
                        .into_response()
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

pub async fn fs_list_handler(
    State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>,
    Query(q): Query<FsQuery>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let user_path = q.path.unwrap_or_else(|| ".".into());
    match safe_path(&state.workspace_root, &user_path) {
        Err(e) => e.into_response(),
        Ok(safe) => {
            let mut entries = Vec::new();
            if let Ok(mut read_dir) = tokio::fs::read_dir(&safe).await {
                while let Ok(Some(entry)) = read_dir.next_entry().await {
                    let meta = entry.metadata().await.ok();
                    entries.push(json!({
                        "name": entry.file_name().to_string_lossy(),
                        "path": entry.path().strip_prefix(&state.workspace_root).unwrap_or(&entry.path()).to_string_lossy(),
                        "is_dir": meta.map(|m| m.is_dir()).unwrap_or(false)
                    }));
                }
            }
            Json(entries).into_response()
        }
    }
}

pub async fn start_server(state: Arc<AppState>) {
    // ⚡ Secure API Routes behind Auth Guard
    let guarded_api = Router::new()
        .route("/task", post(unified_gateway))
        .route("/metrics", get(metrics_handler))
        .route("/fs/read", get(fs_read_handler))
        .route("/fs/write", post(fs_write_handler))
        .route("/fs/list", get(fs_list_handler))
        .route("/vitals", get(vitals_handler::handle_vitals))
        .route("/cluster/nodes", get(cluster_api::list_nodes))
        .route_layer(middleware::from_fn(security::sovereign_auth_guard));

    // ⚡ FIX: Unprotected API Routes (Login) + Merge Guarded Routes
    let api_routes =
        Router::new().route("/auth/login", post(crate::auth::login_handler)).merge(guarded_api);

    let app = Router::new()
        .nest("/api", api_routes)
        .route("/ws/telemetry", get(ws_bridge::ws_telemetry_handler))
        .fallback(static_handler) // ⚡ RustEmbed serves the Svelte build
        .with_state(state);

    let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:3000").await.unwrap();
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}

async fn static_handler(uri: Uri) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let path = uri.path().trim_start_matches('/').to_string();
    let final_path = if path.is_empty() { "index.html".to_string() } else { path };

    let asset = Assets::get(&final_path).or_else(|| Assets::get(&format!("./{}", final_path)));

    match asset {
        Some(content) => {
            let mime = mime_guess::from_path(&final_path).first_or_octet_stream();
            ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, mime.as_ref())], content.data).into_response()
        }
        None => {
            if final_path.contains('.') && !final_path.ends_with(".html") {
                return StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response();
            }
            match Assets::get("index.html").or_else(|| Assets::get("./index.html")) {
                Some(content) => {
                    ([(header::CONTENT_TYPE, "text/html")], content.data).into_response()
                }
                None => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND.into_response(),
            }
        }
    }
}

=========================================
FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/system_monitor.rs 
=========================================
use sysinfo::{CpuRefreshKind, MemoryRefreshKind, RefreshKind, System};

pub struct Vitals {
    pub cpu_load: f32,
    pub ram_gb: f32,
    pub total_ram_gb: f32,
    pub temperature: f32,
}

pub struct SystemMonitor {
    sys: System,
}

impl SystemMonitor {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        let mut sys = System::new_with_specifics(
            RefreshKind::new()
                .with_cpu(CpuRefreshKind::everything())
                .with_memory(MemoryRefreshKind::everything()),
        );
        sys.refresh_all();
        Self { sys }
    }

    fn read_thermal_zone(&self) -> f32 {
        std::fs::read_to_string("/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone1/temp")
            .unwrap_or_else(|_| "45000".to_string())
            .trim()
            .parse::<f32>()
            .unwrap_or(45000.0)
            / 1000.0
    }

    pub fn get_vitals(&mut self) -> Vitals {
        self.sys.refresh_cpu_all();
        self.sys.refresh_memory();

        let cpu_load = self.sys.global_cpu_usage() / 100.0;
        let ram_gb = (self.sys.used_memory() as f32) / 1_073_741_824.0;
        let total_ram_gb = (self.sys.total_memory() as f32) / 1_073_741_824.0;
        let temperature = self.read_thermal_zone();

        Vitals { cpu_load, ram_gb, total_ram_gb, temperature }
    }
}

impl Default for SystemMonitor {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/telemetry_bridge.rs 
=========================================
//! TelemetryBridge — reads real system vitals every 3 s and broadcasts
//! them as a JSON heartbeat that the frontend's store.svelte.ts consumes.
use opentelemetry::{global, propagation::Injector};
use reqwest::header::{HeaderMap, HeaderName, HeaderValue};
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, Mutex};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

/// Carrier adapter for Reqwest / Axum HeaderMaps to support OpenTelemetry propagation.
pub struct HeaderMapInjector<'a> {
    pub headers: &'a mut HeaderMap,
}

impl<'a> Injector for HeaderMapInjector<'a> {
    fn set(&mut self, key: &str, value: String) {
        if let (Ok(name), Ok(val)) =
            (HeaderName::from_bytes(key.as_bytes()), HeaderValue::from_str(&value))
        {
            self.headers.insert(name, val);
        }
    }
}

/// Injects the current W3C traceparent context into outbound HTTP request headers.
pub fn inject_traceparent(headers: &mut HeaderMap) {
    global::get_text_map_propagator(|propagator| {
        propagator
            .inject_context(&opentelemetry::Context::current(), &mut HeaderMapInjector { headers });
    });
}

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [TELEMETRY] Bridge initialised");
}

pub fn spawn(
    tx: broadcast::Sender<String>,
    monitor: Arc<Mutex<nexus_ide_backend::system_monitor::SystemMonitor>>,
    resonance: Arc<std::sync::atomic::AtomicU32>,
) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(3));
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            let vitals = {
                let mut m = monitor.lock().await;
                m.get_vitals()
            };
            let res = resonance.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed) as f32 / 1000.0;
            let active_vmos = nexus_substrate::kernel::get_active_vmos();

            let msg = serde_json::json!({
                "type":        "VITALS",
                "cpu_load":    vitals.cpu_load,
                "ram_gb":      vitals.ram_gb,
                "total_ram_gb": vitals.total_ram_gb,
                "temperature": vitals.temperature,
                "resonance":   res,
                "active_vmos": active_vmos,
            });
            let _ = tx.send(msg.to_string());
        }
    });
}

=========================================
FILE: bins/ide_backend/src/thermal_governor.rs 
=========================================
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicU32, Ordering};
use tokio::time::{interval, Duration};

static THROTTLED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
static PAUSED: AtomicBool = AtomicBool::new(false);
static TEMP_C: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);

pub fn init() {
    tracing::info!(">> [THERMAL] Governor initialised");
}

pub fn is_throttled() -> bool {
    THROTTLED.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}
pub fn is_paused() -> bool {
    PAUSED.load(Ordering::Relaxed)
}
pub fn current_temp() -> f32 {
    TEMP_C.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f32
}

pub fn spawn() {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        let mut components = sysinfo::Components::new();
        loop {
            ticker.tick().await;
            components.refresh();

            let max_temp = components
                .iter()
                .map(|c| c.temperature())
                .filter(|t| t.is_finite())
                .fold(0f32, f32::max);

            TEMP_C.store(max_temp as u32, Ordering::Relaxed);
            THROTTLED.store(max_temp > 70.0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            PAUSED.store(max_temp > 80.0, Ordering::Relaxed);

            if max_temp > 75.0 {
                tracing::warn!("[THERMAL] Temperature {}°C — throttling agent pipeline", max_temp);
            }
        }
    });
}

use crate::server::AppState;
use axum::{extract::State, response::IntoResponse, Json};
use serde_json::json;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub async fn handle_vitals(State(state): State<Arc<AppState>>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let mut monitor = state.monitor.lock().await;
    let v: nexus_ide_backend::system_monitor::Vitals = monitor.get_vitals();
    Json(json!({
        "cpu_load":     v.cpu_load,
        "ram_gb":       v.ram_gb,
        "total_ram_gb": v.total_ram_gb,
        "temperature":  v.temperature,
    }))
}

use std::time::Instant;
use ide_backend::system_monitor::{SystemMonitor, Vitals};

fn main() {
    let mut monitor = SystemMonitor::new();
    let n = 1000;
    let start = Instant::now();
    for _ in 0..n {
        let _ = monitor.get_vitals();
    }
    let duration = start.elapsed();
    println!("Average time per get_vitals: {:?}", duration / n);
}

[dependencies]

FILE: bins/node/src/main.rs 
=========================================
use axum::{
    body::Body,
    extract::{Json, State},
    http::{Request, StatusCode},
    response::IntoResponse,
    routing::{get, post},
    Router,
};
use base64::Engine;
use nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction;
use nexus_runtime_core::wasm_forge::WasmForge;
use nexus_runtime_core::wasm_runtime::WasmRuntime;
use nexus_substrate::kernel::safe_kernel::SafeKernel;
use nexus_substrate::kernel::safe_vmo::SafeVmo;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

// --- State Management ---
struct NodeState {
    vmo_registry: Mutex<HashMap<u32, SafeVmo>>,
}

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoCreateReq {
    size: usize,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct VmoCreateRes {
    handle: u32,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoWriteReq {
    handle: u32,
    offset: usize,
    data: Vec<u8>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoReadReq {
    handle: u32,
    offset: usize,
    size: usize,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ForgeReq {
    code: String,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ForgeRes {
    ok: bool,
    b64: Option<String>,
    error: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct ExecuteReq {
    wasm_b64: String,
    func_name: String,
    input: f32,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct ExecuteRes {
    ok: bool,
    result: Option<f32>,
    error: Option<String>,
}
#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct VmoEntropyReq {
    handle: u32,
}
#[derive(Serialize)]
struct VmoEntropyRes {
    entropy: f32,
}

// --- Middleware: Sovereign Guard ---
async fn auth_guard(req: Request<Body>, next: axum::middleware::Next) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let auth_header = req.headers().get("Authorization").and_then(|v| v.to_str().ok());
    if auth_header == Some("SOVEREIGN") {
        next.run(req).await
    } else {
        (StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED, "Access Denied: SOVEREIGN token required").into_response()
    }
}

// --- Handlers ---
async fn create_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoCreateReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    match SafeKernel::create_vmo(req.size) {
        Ok(handle) => {
            if let Ok(vmo) = SafeVmo::new(req.size) {
                state.vmo_registry.lock().await.insert(handle, vmo);
                (StatusCode::OK, Json(VmoCreateRes { handle })).into_response()
            } else {
                (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "VMO Wrapper failure").into_response()
            }
        }
        Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Kernel Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
    }
}

async fn write_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoWriteReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if let Some(vmo) = registry.get(&req.handle) {
        match vmo.write(req.offset, &req.data) {
            Ok(_) => StatusCode::OK.into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Write Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn read_vmo(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoReadReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if let Some(vmo) = registry.get(&req.handle) {
        let mut buf = vec![0u8; req.size];
        match vmo.read_into(req.offset, &mut buf) {
            Ok(_) => Json(buf).into_response(),
            Err(e) => (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, format!("Read Error: {:?}", e)).into_response(),
        }
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn calculate_entropy(
    State(state): State<Arc<NodeState>>,
    Json(req): Json<VmoEntropyReq>,
) -> impl IntoResponse {
    if req.handle == 0 {
        return (StatusCode::OK, Json(VmoEntropyRes { entropy: 0.0 })).into_response();
    }
    let registry = state.vmo_registry.lock().await;
    if registry.contains_key(&req.handle) {
        let entropy = nexus_substrate::kernel::calculate_entropy(req.handle);
        (StatusCode::OK, Json(VmoEntropyRes { entropy })).into_response()
    } else {
        (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, "VMO handle not found").into_response()
    }
}

async fn synthesize_wasm(Json(req): Json<ForgeReq>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    match WasmForge::synthesize_cell(&req.code).await {
        Ok(b64) => Json(ForgeRes { ok: true, b64: Some(b64), error: None }).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            Json(ForgeRes { ok: false, b64: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) }).into_response()
        }
    }
}

async fn execute_wasm(Json(req): Json<ExecuteReq>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    let bytes = match base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.decode(&req.wasm_b64) {
        Ok(b) => b,
        Err(e) => {
            return Json(ExecuteRes { ok: false, result: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) })
                .into_response()
        }
    };
    match WasmRuntime::run_bytes(&bytes, &req.func_name, req.input) {
        Ok(res) => Json(ExecuteRes { ok: true, result: Some(res), error: None }).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            Json(ExecuteRes { ok: false, result: None, error: Some(e.to_string()) }).into_response()
        }
    }
}

// ⚡ PHASE 3: The magic endpoint that receives LLM actions from the Duet and executes them locally on the Node's filesystem.
async fn execute_physical(Json(action): Json<AgentAction>) -> impl IntoResponse {
    // ⚡ FIX: ToolExecutor was deleted during the Executor Unification pass.
    // As `nexus_node` acts as a remote satellite without a true Memory Palace
    // or VmoState right now, we spin up a localized RoboticExecutor instance.
    let (tx, _) = tokio::sync::broadcast::channel(1);
    let mut executor = nexus_runtime_core::executor::RoboticExecutor::new(
        String::new(), // Edge nodes don't need the API key
        tx,
    );

    // Mock up the substrate dependencies for the edge node
    let memory = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::memory_cortex::MemoryCortex::new(Arc::new(
        tokio::sync::RwLock::new(
            nexus_substrate::memory::MemoryPalace::new(":memory:").await.unwrap(),
        ),
    )));
    let vmo_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(nexus_runtime_core::shared_state::VmoState::default()));
    let dist = Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::distributed_scheduler::DistributedScheduler::new(
        Arc::new(nexus_runtime_core::cluster::ClusterMesh::new()),
    ));

    
        let motor_action = match action {
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::ExecuteCommand { command, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::ExecuteCommand { command },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::WriteFile { path, content, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::WriteFile { path, content },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::ReadFile { path, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::ReadFile { path },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::ReadFileChunk { path, start_line, max_lines, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::ReadFileChunk { path, start_line, max_lines },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::SearchCode { path, query, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::SearchCode { path, query },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::DiffPatch { path, search, replace },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::PatchFunction { path, function_name, new_body },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::SearchMemory { query, .. } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::SearchMemory { query },
            nexus_runtime_core::agent_protocol::AgentAction::Halt { reasoning } => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::Halt { reasoning },
            _ => nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorAction::Halt { reasoning: "unmapped".to_string() },
        };
        let motor_cmd = nexus_runtime_core::motor_cortex::MotorCommand {
                command_id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),
                intent_id: uuid::Uuid::new_v4(),
                goal_id: None,
                action: motor_action,
                capability: nexus_runtime_core::policy::Capability::ExecuteSystemCall, // Human override
                expected_outcome: "CLI_DIRECT_INVOCATION".to_string(),
                created_at: 0,
            };
            match executor.execute_motor_command(motor_cmd, memory, vmo_state, dist).await {
        Ok(obs) => (StatusCode::OK, obs).into_response(),
        Err(e) => {
            (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, format!("ACTION_FAILED: {}", e)).into_response()
        }
    }
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt::init();
    let state = Arc::new(NodeState { vmo_registry: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()) });

    let app = Router::new()
        .route("/api/vmo/create", post(create_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/write", post(write_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/read", post(read_vmo))
        .route("/api/vmo/entropy", post(calculate_entropy))
        .route("/api/action/physical", post(execute_physical))
        .route("/api/forge/synthesize", post(synthesize_wasm))
        .route("/api/forge/execute", post(execute_wasm))
        .route("/", get(|| async { "Sovereign Muscle Node Online" }))
        .layer(axum::middleware::from_fn(auth_guard))
        .with_state(state);

    let listener = tokio::net::TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:8080").await.unwrap();
    tracing::info!(">> [NODE] Sovereign Muscle active on port 8080");
    axum::serve(listener, app).await.unwrap();
}
